**[pbu21-0118]** — Reader context: Urges planning support and contact numbers before crisis.

> Please contact your midwife team if you’re struggling or go to A&E. If you’re prone to MH issues, make sure your partner and loved ones know what to look out for. I started on antidepressants when my daughter was about 4w. I’ve always refused them in the past. But I was so scared that at some point I might be so sick I would hurt her without meaning to. Have the numbers you need ready and to hand before you need them.

**[pbu21-0123]** — Reader context: Orders crutches and takes over housework while pregnancy pain persists.

> We're now pretty sure that it's PGP / SPD, as the pain has not gone away for 2 weeks. The last few days in particular have been horrible for her. The pain has been really bad at times. I'm really concerned about her, and I'm helping as much as possible to make sure that she doesn't have to lift a finger round the house (although I do need to go into the office 3-4 days a week so won't always be able to be around). I've ordered some crutches arriving tomorrow which I'm hoping might help.

**[pbu21-0124]** — Reader context: Worries loss of independence will compound the physical pain.

> More importantly I'm worried about the mental toll this could have on her. She loves her independence, and going on walks / hanging out with friends... Especially with the autumn and winter arriving it might get tougher to get out and about.

**[pbu21-0128]** — Reader context: Requests earlier induction to preserve capacity for labor.

> I want to request to be induced at 38 weeks just because of the physical toll this is all taking on me. Im in triage almost weekly sometimes twice weekly for reduced movements and im worried that if I continue down this path by the time im induced at 39 weeks, my body is just going to be too exhausted to labour.

**[pbu21-0129]** — Reader context: Delayed discovery of retained tissue after bleeding since birth.

> So I just found out today I have retained products left from pregnancy/birth and i have to go in for a D&C today!! I am almost 12 weeks post partum. Just posting this as heads up for others to keep an eye on your symptoms and tell your dr! I've been bleeding off and on since, thought I got my period 2 times, and had what I thought was BV. Turns out it is not! I've had pain in my uterus and a foul odor. My dr sent me for an ultrasound just to rule out retained products and sure enough... there are some. Please watch for symptoms and don't be afraid to call the dr and be annoying!! Wish me luck❤️😬

**[pbu21-0134]** — Reader context: Describes catheterization, incontinence and wheelchair use after birth.

> The worse thing is the incontinence.  After birth pee fell right out of me and I ended up having a catheter for a day.  Recovery was very painful and I had to get a wheelchair after baby appt because I was in so much pain.

**[pbu21-0135]** — Reader context: Prior injury drives questions about a future section.

> Would a c section for a 2nd birth be a little easier?  Also with the damage I had, idk if a 2nd birth could make the issues worse....and that is super sad

**[pbu21-0138]** — Reader context: Postpartum bleeding after activity must wait for a weekday reply.

> I had the normal actual bleeding for the first week, then lochia serosa for probably 10 days and after that it seemed to have progressed to lochia alba but it's really mucusy. A couple of times in the past week and a half, there's been bloody streaks in it after a little bit of activity (vacuuming last week, running a bunch of errands this week). I assume this is normal but this is my first time being postpartum. I did message my doctor but it's the weekend so I won't hear back for a couple of days. Nothing is serious enough for me to consider going into the hospital either, I don't feel unwell or anything like that, just like I overdid it a little.

**[pbu21-0165]** — Reader context: Searches for meaningful interpretation of a genetic screening result.

> The test said that she is at risk for something called DiGeorge syndrome aka 22q11.2 deletion syndrome. Wanted to see if anyone else here has heard of this and what it means for my little girl. I've done some research on Google and it says that it's a very varied syndrome with some people having certain symptoms and others not so much. Hoping just to get some more insight as to help my daughter best! Thank you all!!

**[pbu21-0169]** — Reader context: Hospital worker considers quitting after requests to change physical duties receive little response.

> I've been very transparent with my boss about these issues, I sent her a message yesterday outlining these issues and just kind of trying to give her a heads up that my job duties may need to change or I may just not be able to work there anymore. The only response I got was "text ( other managers name)" and I asked for the other managers number, I don't have it because I've never had to reach out to her, and never got a response. It really infuriated me, I've worked there for over a year, been a full time employee and you can't at least pretend to have half a shred of compassion? After my appointment Monday I'm tempted to go in, turn my badge in and just say sorry, I'm done, my baby's health and my health is more important than this.

**[pbu21-0171]** — Reader context: Booked private imaging to avoid another emergency-department wait.

> This time the pain was on my left side, which scared me even more because I worried it might be my pancreas. I booked a private ultrasound before heading to the hospital. I just couldn’t face another long wait in A&E. It turned out my gallbladder was worse, and the stones had started moving into the bile ducts. I was told to go to the ER immediately.

**[pbu21-0218]** — Reader context: Yoga, walking, work and supportive colleagues do not resolve distress.

> I promise you I do pregnancy yoga thrice a week and go for walks and also working full time so I meet meet my amazing and supportive  colleagues. Gotta find a therapist, tribe and learn to manoeuvre this new life

**[pbu21-0224]** — Reader context: Anxiety near due date shifts preference away from avoiding induction.

> My next appointment will be tomorrow morning, where I’ve been promised a membrane sweep and the ability to schedule an induction. I was hoping to avoid an induction, but I’m really starting to get too anxious to keep going on like this.

**[pbu21-0255]** — Reader context: Wants to try again before results explaining second-trimester loss return.

> I had a second trimester miscarriage (delivered at 20 weeks discovered at my 20 week scan that baby stopped growing at around 18 weeks).  
> We want to try again but we have not yet heard back from the hospital regarding test results (placenta was sent off for testing).   
> I have not yet ovulated I don’t think and my post partum bleeding just finished.   
> How long did anyone else in my boat or similar experience wait to try again?   
> Would you try again now or wait for testing?   
> I am hopeful that it won’t happen again and it was a random occurrence but I don’t know I’m scared at the same time.   
> At the end of the day I believe what’s meant to happen will happen by the will of God but I just want to know what others experiences have been.   
> Thanks in advance.

**[pbu21-0256]** — Reader context: Questions prescribed contraception because wants to conceive sooner.

> I had miscarriage 2 weeks ago and the OB prescribed me birth control to avoid getting pregnant until 3 months as she recommended, but I’m hesitant to take the birth control because I want to conceive as soon as possible. Is it safe to get pregnant sooner after that miscarriage happened? Or should I just take the birth control?

**[pbu21-0257]** — Reader context: Feels pain care is reduced to Tylenol limits without alternatives.

> Now that im pregnant I can only take tylenol and the pain is AGGRESSIVE. I think the most frustrating thing here is the medical community couldn't give two fucks about pregnant women's pain. They tell me the safe amount of tylenol to take and send me on my way. Theres no alternative options. Just a "Deal with your pain glad its you and not me"

**[pbu21-0258]** — Reader context: Constant pain and sleeplessness undermine mental health.

> Im honestly so tired because I cant sleep. Im still in the first trimester but my mental health is tanking extremely fast because of this. I just want my baby to be safe but God the cost is really high for me. I would love to just be nauseous or just be tired. But nah, im in constant pain. I hope this journey is worth it because im on the cusp of giving up the ghost.

**[pbu21-0272]** — Reader context: Starting again after a second loss feels insurmountable.

> We’re working to get in with a reproductive endocrinologist to do some more specialized testing and talk about our next options. I am so worried we will never have a successful pregnancy and never get to have a baby. I am so devastated by this loss, especially on the heels of losing my first pregnancy. Starting over feels like a crushing, insurmountable hill to climb again.

**[pbu21-0281]** — Reader context: Disrupted water routine causes meltdown and shame during pregnancy.

> I am autistic. I had a bad meltdown this morning because my routine was disturbed. I always drink bottled water so I can track how much I drink and it is familiar to me. I am currently 30 weeks pregnant and can’t lift anything heavy. We ran out of water last night so, I asked my husband to come with me very early this morning to the store to help me. He didn’t get home til very late last night so he did not want to get up and needed more sleep before he works another long shift today. I took that TERRIBLY. I started panicking and my husband suggested I just go buy a gallon of water. I didnt want to buy a gallon of water. Straying from the normal routine felt like the end of the world. We ended up having a really heated argument and I had a terrible meltdown where I was hitting my hands on my head and thighs, screaming and crying. My baby is perfectly fine. I didn’t hit my belly but, now I feel immense shame and regret for what I did. I couldnt control myself. I know I am going to be a great parent but right now I feel like the worst. Please say anything negative. I just need support. I am not in danger to myself or anyone else and would never intentionally do anything to hurt myself at this point in my life.

**[pbu21-0293]** — Reader context: Seeks another hospital and demands bloodwork after perceived dismissal.

> About 8 years ago I had two miscarriages back to back. I just found out last Friday I was around 5 weeks. They did my hcg levels that were around 30,000. I started bleeding that Sunday prior to me find out I was pregnant I started bleeding. I went in for an appointment Monday and I asked about another HCG test and she said that wouldn't necessarily tell her anything. Before the appointment my symptoms started to fade away. But I went to a different hospital emergency room and the doctor made sure they drew blood to check. Now I have been spotting for the last two days. Not soaking up a pad and mild cramps. I dont have my next appointment until the 21st and my mind is going crazy. I can barely focus on anything. I just messaged my doctor demanding that I have a test ran for my hcg levels. Reddit has really been helping me this past week. Any advice?

**[pbu21-0295]** — Reader context: Chronic pain relief conflicts with pregnancy concerns and clinician’s advice.

> Also what the f\*\*\* can I do going forward without my hot baths and flower!!! It’s seriously my life line for pain management; I’ve tried every damn trick in the book with 0 luck on helping my pain.   
> My current holisticy OBGYN doctor said she’d rather have me consume a small amount of marijuana and keep my food down and not be in constant pain, but I feel so conflicted. What do I do. Will my baby even be okay?

**[pbu21-0296]** — Reader context: Usual prescribed anti-nausea medication no longer handles combined symptoms.

> It’s also a huge relief to hear about your two healthy boys being here even though you took baths throughout your pregnancy! One of my friends is 20 weeks pregnant and highly advised against it, so I got really nervous once I found out. I do also have a prescription for zofran which usually keeps my “normal” (not pregnancy) related nausea at bay, but hasn’t been able to keep up with my now pregnant+normal nausea and vomiting. I’m going to have to keep trying new things to see if anything will work because I’ve been so dang sick but don’t want to smoke now!

**[pbu21-0302]** — Reader context: Unavailable iron treatment and limited supplement response intensify fear before induction.

> I was never able to get in for an iron transfusion due to high demand. My numbers didn’t improve much after taking a supplement and I’m terrified of hemorrhaging during birth. My hemoglobin level the last it was checked was **14.5 and ferritin 11.**

**[pbu21-0307]** — Reader context: Bereavement reduces concentration for registry decisions and newborn preparation.

> I am trying to get the essentials where the baby sleeps and lays to be greenguard gold certified and as nontoxic as possible.   
> I’m worried about buying things that I don’t need as we have a small home and live minimally. With her loss, it’s just been really hard to concentrate on getting this registry together. I have only had time to work and just trying to keep myself healthy and read about preparing for labor and how to actually care for the newborn when they come.

**[pbu21-0310]** — Reader context: Seeks earlier induction while online claims and movement changes extend fear.

> Now I have intense anxiety about baby being tangled into his cord or something and I can’t shake it. To top it all off, I had asked the OB that I’ve been seeing (my provider is on leave), if there was anyway to get induced at 37 weeks vs 39 weeks due to maternal anxiety, and she told me that my MFM has to make that determination..I don’t see them for another week and a half. & then I saw something in another subreddit that said that excessive hiccuping from baby is a warning sign & he’s had the hiccups 2-3 times a day for the past few days. To add, it seems like baby boy has changed his sleep wake pattern? While I was actively working he would be awake much more during the day (I have an office job), but over the past week or so, it seems like he’s much more active at night around 10-11. So now I am this complete ball of anxiety and nothing I do can shake it, I used to be able to get 9-10 hours of sleep uninterrupted and now I’m lucky if I can get half of that.

**[pbu21-0319]** — Reader context: Starts disability leave and preparation while uncertain about newborn prognosis.

> What a wild ride this has been, I’m now out of work on STD and will be working on our hospital bag and nursery while attempting to prioritize rest and remaining stress free. We have no idea what this means for our son once he’s born, the flutter can disappear on its own outside of the womb or we could be on medicine for awhile.

**[pbu21-0322]** — Reader context: Unexplained recurrent losses make waiting until eleven weeks feel unacceptable.

> So for some context, I have two living children, with different dads, 12 years apart, fast forward to my now husband, last year, I experienced a missed miscarriage, and 6 months later I experienced a blighted ovum. We did tons of testing, we sent samples from dnc, blood work, etc dna results came back normal, so did all of the test, unexplained abnormality, but still, diagnosed with unexplained reoccurring pregnancy loss.  Now fast forward 9 weeks after my d&c, no cycle in between, I am pregnant again, and so scared. And the doctor wants to wait till I am 11 weeks along to even see me, and I don’t feel comfortable waiting that long. I’m considering asking for routine blood work up until then, as that was how we discovered the blighted ovum last time. I need advice, or even share your stories.

**[pbu21-0339]** — Reader context: Fears disclosing distress created more risk and administrative pressure.

> I don’t know what to do, I told the midwife how I felt that I just needed support and advice and not another thing to stress over, I don’t want to worry about social services involvement, I don’t want to have to worry about more back lash from him and I feel like I did the wrong thing telling them how much of a difficult time I’ve been having now. Am I just being a hormonal, overthinking mess?

**[pbu21-0342]** — Reader context: Medication and talk therapy feel insufficient.

> Talk therapy and antidepressants don’t seem to be helping enough right now, and I’m scared by how low I’ve become.

**[pbu21-0344]** — Reader context: Fainting from period pain shapes fear about labor and choice of birth companion.

> I want my mum in the delivery room but also don’t. I want her support but I also want to experience this intimate moment with just my husband. I genuinely can’t decide. The thought of birth is terrifying but also so beautiful. I just hate not knowing how it’ll go. My mum has done it 4 times without epidural so part of me feels like I’d feel better with her being there. I’m so conflicted. I know that many people have positive birth stories but being in pain like that doesn’t sound positive in any aspect. I think about it not being so bad since women choose to do it multiple times but I can’t even stand my period pain. I pass out from period pain due to endometriosis and adenomyosis. If I pass out from pain during labour, how will I push baby out?

**[pbu21-0367]** — Reader context: New symptoms at 39 weeks threaten an expected vaginal birth.

> I had valacyclovir in the house and started taking this morning and put diluted tea tree oil where is hurts but I am panicking that being this close to birth I will have to have a csection. I am terrified enough to do a normal birth but this was so unexpected I’m freaking out. I will see my OB in like 36 hours so I will mention it to them but looking for advice, hope, reassurance, anything. Literally everything has gone so smoothly until now.

**[pbu21-0399]** — Reader context: Apprentice fears losing job after disclosure despite knowing discrimination rules exist.

> My biggest worry right now is my job. I feel like everything is riding on my performance at work and if I get fired or not once I reveal my pregnancy(which is going to have to be soon, with how far along I already am). I know there’s laws in place to prevent discrimination but I can’t help but not trust those. Now to disclose my tricky situation, I’m a female plumbing apprentice, not only that, but I started in june without knowing I was already a few weeks pregnant by then. Anddd… to worsen it, my uncle is in a sort of management position there and is the one who recommended me. There are a few things that worsen it even more! I like my uncle, I don’t think he dislikes me but we aren’t the closest, this is mainly because i’m not a very social person even among family. I feel guilty in a way, especially because i’m very grateful for him recommending me, that i’m pregnant because it will make things awkward for him, not just me.

**[pbu21-0400]** — Reader context: Home-birth midwife cost and fear of being judged block planning.

> My birth plan is cause for concern also. There is pretty much no plan and I haven’t taken any courses or anything even though i’m this far along. The baby is perfectly healthy and I have another checkup tomorrow. My boyfriend wants me to have a natural home birth, and I also want that, it’s just that a midwife won’t be covered by my insurance, and it will cost about $4\~5k. Of course many of them offer payment plans, which helps a lot, but it’s stressful thinking about how I will need to pay for rent, food, gas, insurance, all the other baby necessities, etc. soon as well. While his mom will help a little, I’ll be paying for most of everything as I’m the one with a full time job(again, praying I don’t get fired). Back to the midwife problem, aside from the cost, i’m also very worried I won’t be able to find one I feel very comfortable with. I feel like i’ll feel judged by most of them as I have some slightly unconventional requests(nothing really weird but also not completely sorted out just yet) for my birth.

**[pbu21-0401]** — Reader context: Expects to miss FMLA eligibility and worries about time with newborn.

> The only thing I think I forgot to mention until now is how i’m also worried that I won’t have much time to spend with my child, especially after giving birth because, like I said, I work overtime most weeks. I also won’t qualify for FMLA until a few months after I give birth, and even then that only gets me a few unpaid weeks off. Lol, i’m very conflicted on how i’ll be treated at work once I disclose my pregnancy! I feel like they’ll either be very supporting or i’ll be fired, no in between🥹

**[pbu21-0404]** — Reader context: Service availability changes expected procedure and creates fertility fears.

> PP told me at the time of when they have availability for surgery, I’ll likely need a dilation and evacuation surgery rather than just a vacuum aspiration, and I’m nervous about the small risk of uterine perforation and cervical injury that comes w a D and E. I don’t want to risk my future fertility.  
> I wish that if I was going to terminate, I’d have done it at 5 weeks, but the idea of terminating was never on my mind— we were excited and discussing names initially. At 5 weeks I thought it was probably just a chemical pregnancy and I didn’t get confirmation of pregnancy from a doctor until I was about 9 weeks along.

**[pbu21-0411]** — Reader context: First full-term pregnancy brings uncertainty after assessment sends parent home.

> I’m currently 39 weeks 6 days pregnant I’ll be 40 tomorrow . I’ve been having contractions and some light bleeding this morning. But I’m not soaking any pads I when to the hospital they pretty much send me home . Contractions are 8 to 10min right now apart I’ve dilated not even 1cm .has anybody had light bleeding and contractions like this far apart ? They also said baby heart beat min was normal . They tested to see if my water had broken that was negative  . The pain is a little intense. they say I could be like this for a couple days or maybe another week.. its my first full term pregnancy.  The last time one was ectopic so I don’t know what to expect this time .

**[pbu21-0413]** — Reader context: Multiple home remedies fail; unsure emergency care is warranted.

I’ve had two rolaids, two tums, milk, ginger tea, I’ve walked probably over a mile outside. I took a warm shower. I literally have zero relief and in so much pain. It’s like an upper crushing back pain from middle of back to middle of shoulder blades. Google says the er, but I feel like that’s overkill?

**[pbu32-0013]** — Reader context: Pleural effusion found at anatomy scan; awaiting specialist appointment.

**[pbu32-0076]** — Reader context: Seeking high-risk patients' hospital experiences rather than nearest maternity unit.

>

**[pbu32-0104]** — Reader context: Four weeks postpartum with ongoing headaches; seeks peers after already consulting midwife.

**[pbu32-0105]** — Reader context: Physically symptom-free pregnancy includes insomnia and new anxiety.

**[pbu32-0107]** — Reader context: Follow-up accepts clinician's PPD/PPA assessment and disability extension.

> Now a few days out and talking more with my husband I’m really glad she did say something and I’m getting some more support.

**[pbu32-0119]** — Reader context: After losses, asks for HCG and progesterone tests discovered through Reddit.

**[pbu32-0135]** — Reader context: Unconfirmed pregnancy; lack of parental financial support shapes fear.

**[pbu32-0145]** — Reader context: Different prescription from options discussed prompts online risk search and hesitation.

**[pbu32-0157]** — Reader context: Coworker's account of examinations and instruments triggers fear before first gynecological exam.

> I genuinely think I can see this through with the right support. But if I'm keeping this pregnancy I need the support of the father and I don't want to force him.

**[pbu32-0190]** — Reader context: Pushes for more HCG tests to gain advance warning, not simply repeated reassurance.

**[pbu32-0260]** — Reader context: High preterm-labor risk; requested ultrasound unavailable and fear of seeming foolish delays calling.

**[pbu32-0283]** — Reader context: Devastating first-trimester sickness motivates explicit request to compare pregnancy with postpartum.

**[pbu32-0285]** — Reader context: After stopping anxiety and ADHD medication, work functioning declines during pregnancy after loss.

**[pbu32-0288]** — Reader context: Hospitalized with unexplained fever; proposed amniocentesis and possible extreme prematurity trigger self-blame.

**[pbu32-0294]** — Reader context: Spotting in second pregnancy; nurse's uncertainty gives little immediate reassurance.

**[pbu32-0311]** — Reader context: Reports vomiting during panic attacks being misread as nausea, followed by OB dismissal.

**[pbu32-0312]** — Reader context: Author's account of severe insomnia and failed access to treatment; usual suggestions feel mismatched.

### A professional asks how to support bereaved parents

This is the postpartum doula’s question, retained as an other-side voice rather than a parent’s purchase request.

**[pbu32-0047]** — Reader context: Postpartum doula asks how support should continue after client baby's death.

> Did you continue to check in from time to time, or did you give the family space unless they reached out? Were there things you did that the family later appreciated, or things you wish you had done differently? I want to support them in whatever way is most respectful and helpful.

## Provenance and independence

The ur08 ledger identifies crossposts of one narrative and additions to already quoted posts; the ur32 ledger also identifies related crossposts in other assignments. Different passages or IDs do not turn those accounts into independent support. The father’s stillbirth sequence below is one account: [pbu08-0156] [pbu08-0157] [pbu08-0158] [pbu08-0159] [pbu08-0160] [pbu08-0161] [pbu08-0162] [pbu08-0163].

The polished, crossposted home-remedy account is retained as one author’s claimed experience: [pbu21-0119] [pbu21-0120]. Its treatment claims are not corroborated by appearing twice. The public-policy announcement is [pbu21-0125]; the unverified celebrity-publicity reaction is [pbu32-0081]; the physician’s docusate criticism is [pbu32-0331]. Keep their source roles and uncertainty.

Programmatic comparison found no identical complete quote fields within this share. That is a text check, not an independence claim. The ledger warnings above still apply. Teenage ages and gestational weeks must also remain separate; the clothing and shower-planning questions are ordinary preparation evidence.

## Complete evidence appendix

Every record not already reproduced in full appears here, grouped by its primary pattern for retrieval and ordered by source ID. Full context and wording are retained, including short passages whose meaning depends on their recorded context. Cross-references in the pattern sections can point to records with a different primary grouping.

### Additional evidence for pattern 1: After loss, body signals and reassuring milestones can recall danger rather than settle fear.

**[pbu08-0066]** — Reader context: After consecutive losses asks whether to try immediately and seeks hope.

> Wondering if anyone has stories of having a healthy pregnancy post MMC and chemical? I'm trying to decide if we should try again right away (which is what I'd prefer) or if it's better to have another cycle? And in general, just looking for hope.

**[pbu08-0068]** — Reader context: Loss of symptoms leads to an emergency visit and a silent miscarriage diagnosis.

> found out i was 8 weeks even, my boyfriend & i do long distance, & i just so happened to be at his house & i never really had pregnancy symptoms but only my boobs would hurt  but i didnt feel symptoms anymore so i went to the er was there for about 4 hours to be told i had a silent miscarriage. first time mom & im truly heartbroken. but i wanted to come here & say i hope everyone has  a safe & healthy pregnancy!!!

**[pbu08-0170]** — Reader context: Looks forward to induction despite a hard pregnancy and a previous loss.

> I’m scheduled to be induced April 9th and honestly I can’t wait. While this pregnancy has been super hard on me I wouldn’t have it any other way. At times I did feel down or so over this pregnancy but he is my rainbow baby. I had a a miscarriage back in 2018 and I have 1 son which is 18 (yes a huge age gap I know 😂 ).

**[pbu08-0180]** — Reader context: Doctor's lack of concern does not calm fear of losing a wanted pregnancy.

> Found out I am 4 weeks and 2 days pregnant and I am very concerned about miscarriage because I really want this baby. My doctor is not concerned but I am filled with anxiety.

**[pbu08-0281]** — Reader context: Cautious happiness is undercut by assuming another pregnancy will fail.

> We got a positive test after trying after 1 cycle post miscarriage… feeling cautiously happy, nervous, worried, scared… it was so traumatic and we want a baby so bad but I’m already basically assuming it just won’t work out with this one either… any advice for being pregnant after miscarriages?

**[pbu21-0211]** — Reader context: An early reassurance scan is in the building where prior loss was diagnosed.

> I have an early ultrasound appointment tomorrow and it’s at the same building when I found out I was having a mmc. My ob offered it to me since she couldn’t get me in for the early first trimester visit for another 10 days.

I’ve got whiplash and concussion, but my main fear has been losing the pregnancy. I’ve miscarried before, and I had no bleeding yesterday, but this evening I have pink discharge and cramping. I’m praying it’ll stop and the pregnancy will be safe and healthy. Any positive stories??

**[pbu32-0124]** — Reader context: Pregnant before first period after miscarriage; fears both excitement and emotional distance.

**[pbu32-0139]** — Reader context: Spotting persists after OB suggests water and rest.

**[pbu32-0163]** — Reader context: Nearly five weeks after prior loss; distraction gives only brief respite.

**[pbu32-0170]** — Reader context: Six weeks three days pregnant after symptomatic pregnancy ended in missed miscarriage.

**[pbu32-0255]** — Reader context: Treats strength of anxious feeling as possible warning of repeat loss.

### Additional evidence for pattern 2: Early pregnancy can mean weeks of unresolved viability, discrepant dates and contradictory results.

**[pbu08-0032]** — Reader context: Dating discrepancy recalls the beginning of a previous miscarriage.

**[pbu08-0063]** — Reader context: Confused whether current bleeding counts as the cycle to wait before trying again.

Can anyone help me with dating there is no period to go off of bc of miscarriage

**[pbu21-0314]** — Reader context: Prior ectopic pregnancies make small differences between lab results frightening.

> Hello so I found out I’m 5 weeks pregnant I got bloodwork done at the lab it was 6556.0 and ended up at the er there’s was 6193.7 I been spotting (tmi) literally a speck on a liner n nothing when I wipe this was for 3 days. Now nothing. On said lab n er possibly sentient I guess readers to get results so don’t panic ( yea ok 😔) I’m scared because I have a hx of 2 ectopic pregnancies in the past. Any how has any been through this and ended up with a full term pregnancy and healthy baby? Wishing everyone all the best and sprinkling yall with baby dust 🧚 ❤️

**[pbu21-0349]** — Reader context: Weekly scans continue as long as heartbeat remains despite absent growth.

**[pbu21-0350]** — Reader context: Reassuring line progression does not outweigh concern about discharge.

It’s not heavy or consistent. I’m only 4 weeks on the dot so ik it’s normal, how and when do you stop worrying? I had a chemical pregnancy a year ago and I keep assuming that’s what’s going to happen.

**[pbu21-0374]** — Reader context: Repeated calls after worsening bleeding still lead to waiting for scheduled scan.

> Hi all! So I'm currently 5w6d pregnant with my first. Me and my husband managed to fall pregnant on our first try and all was going well until a couple days ago I noticed a bit of pink/brown discharge after going to the toilet. Thought nothing of it but then started to see some streaks of red the following day. I contacted 111 which directed me back to my GP who called the local EPU. He said that they didn't seemed too concerned as it was intermittent and to call the EPU if it got worse. They had booked me in for a scan for Thursday as it is the earliest they can do. Then at about 4am this morning when I went to pee, the toilet paper was bright red. It's progressively got heavier throughout the day with mild period like cramps, very small clots. It probably fills quarter/half a pad every couple hours, like a period. Called EPU again and they said as long as I'm not soaking through pads within an hour and crippling pain, it's just wait and see until Thursday.

**[pbu21-0378]** — Reader context: Persistent spotting leaves uncertainty despite corrected thyroid levels and positive bloodwork.

> So I went ahead and did pregnancy tests, and they all came back positive, and my doctor did a blood test, and it turned out to be positive. Now i am just very concerned; my last pregnancy ended up being a miscarriage at 8 weeks (I had thyroid issues, but now my levels are excellent). I am concerned bc i am on day 6 of spotting; I see it when i wipe, and the color didn't change. Has anyone experienced this and turned out to be fine?

**[pbu21-0389]** — Reader context: Mixed reading leaves author wanting direction about bleeding and urgent care.

> Am I miscarrying? Is it implantation bleeding? Is it a chemical pregnancy? Am I being dramatic? Pls help 😭 I don’t want to assume the worst, but I’m so scared and don’t know what to do or expect. I also don’t want to go to urgent care for them not even be able to help me or see anything since it’s so early on in the pregnancy. I’ve done so much reading and have seen both good and bad outcomes, but I just need some sense of direction.

**[pbu21-0391]** — Reader context: Unclear scan explanation and delayed report prompt fear despite no symptoms of loss.

> Just had my dating ultrasound at exactly 9 weeks. The tech couldn’t find anything on a regular ultrasound, so ended up doing a vaginal. She asked a lot of questions about if I had any pain or bleeding, if my periods are regular, etc. She ended up telling me she could tell that I’m pregnant, but “can’t make anything else out.” I do have a retroverted uterus, which I did tell her. She said she’d have to send the images to the doctor, who will do a report and reach out to me in a day or two. She did not straight out tell me there was no heartbeat or anything like that, but I feel like that’s what she meant. I have had zero signs of miscarriage. This is my second pregnancy, and I had no complications at all in the first.   
> Has this happened to anyone else? Am I right to be panicking? Or is there a chance the baby is still totally fine?

**[pbu21-0414]** — Reader context: Period-like cramps reactivate fear before scheduled reassurance scan.

> I’m 4 weeks 6 days and having some light cramping in my pelvis. I know this can be totally normal and very common but I swear it feels just like my periods about to start. If I didn’t have a positive test I’d be sure my period would be coming in the next day or so. I had a MMC last year so anyrhing like this makes me panic massively! I’ve another week to go before I can get a reassurance scan and my anxiety is through the roof! Anyone else experience the same?

**[pbu32-0017]** — Reader context: Bleeding after workout triggers tests and anxious interpretation of ranges.

**[pbu32-0094]** — Reader context: Three prior miscarriages; waiting ten days after inconclusive third scan.

> Pregnant 5 weeks after d&c test out and went and got blood work beta was 2 almost 3 weeks test today because been very nauseous sore boobs.

**[pbu32-0247]** — Reader context: Irregular dates and gestational sac without visible baby lead to repeat tests.

**[pbu32-0250]** — Reader context: New positive after prior negative tests following abortion ten weeks earlier.

**[pbu32-0313]** — Reader context: Online search links one-sided cramps and shoulder pain to ectopic pregnancy.

> When looking it up it says there are signs of an ectopic pregnancy. Should I be worried? Or have you guys felt this before

> Could the scan be wrong ? My whole house is sleeping and i finally cried a little but im just not truly convinced that i have lost this baby.

### Additional evidence for pattern 3: Monitoring, stories, apps and AI can either help or intensify checking, and parents want different forms of reassurance.

**[pbu08-0117]** — Reader context: After missed miscarriage, prefers real experiences to repeated searching about recurrence.

> my partner and i had our first scan today to find out there was no heartbeat, they said i had a missed miscarriage 3 weeks ago. we’ve been crying a lot, but now we’re wondering if it’s still possible to try again in the future.
> 
> this was technically our second pregnancy, our first had to be terminated for reasons i won’t go into, but my silly overthinking brain is convinced that now we will never be able to have a successful full term pregnancy.
> 
> i’ve been googling a lot, reasons why this happens, the chances of it happening again etc, but i feel like it would be better to hear it from actual people if that’s okay. thank you <3

**[pbu08-0271]** — Reader context: Reduced soreness leads to internet searching despite a reassuring scan.

> This morning I woke up and noticed my breasts weren’t nearly as sore as they have been. I’m pretty sure this has happened before and that symptoms can fluctuate, but of course I ended up going down an internet rabbit hole about missed miscarriages and now I’m feeling really anxious.

**[pbu08-0309]** — Reader context: Hearing diarrhea can signal miscarriage turns a brief symptom into fear.

> TMI 🚨ofc i’m freaking myself out but last night (one time) and today (one time) had diarrhea and then i somehow heard it’s a sign of miscarriage. has anyone else had diarrhea at 20w pregnant, im not feeling sick or anything else just going to the bathroom

**[pbu08-0404]** — Reader context: Searches recurrent-loss statistics and considers hiding pregnancy to protect the partner from disappointment.

> At first I felt a buzz of excitement and joy as I thought it would take months to happen again. But now I'm spiralling and googling statistics on back to back miscarriages likelihood, wondering if I should keep it to myself so my partner isn't crushed again if it doesn't work.

**[pbu21-0048]** — Reader context: Tracks conception while grieving the pregnancy’s lost timeline.

> My husband and I are on our TTC journey after an MMC at 10 weeks, and at the end of May 2026, I am currently in my first proper cycle since, and I am around 9DPO (negative tests). - for conext i had my IUD removed in Feb and started trying in March and got pregnant on the first go, so to be in this situation is heartbreaking. I am tracking Ovulation via tests, Oura Ring, and BBT thermometer. I know i am only offically 1 cycle in but i am starting to loose hope, each week that goes by is another week i would of been further along. I was due Dec 26 and would be around 20 weeks by now.  I would love to hear some advice on how long it took you after MC.

**[pbu21-0103]** — Reader context: Confusion about pregnancy dating changes the perceived reassurance milestone.

> I was getting excited last night as I thought I was nearly out of the first trimester so I can stop worrying about miscarriage so much but my husband pointed out that I'm technically two weeks behind!

**[pbu21-0329]** — Reader context: Day-to-day test-line darkness becomes a miscarriage signal.

> My husband and I are 4w3d along and my pregnancy test strips today showed a lighter line than the day before. Realistically, could I be miscarrying or losing the baby or should I not worry just yet?

**[pbu21-0337]** — Reader context: Restricts mental investment until pregnancy feels safe to accept.

I want to be happy and excited but aside of taking prenatal vitamins and watching my caffeine intake, I am trying to proceed with my life as it is currently until I can feel safe accepting that this pregnancy will stick. I’m trying not to think about it much until it’s worth thinking about if that makes sense.

**[pbu21-0360]** — Reader context: Names symptom checking and impending doom after loss.

> All of it - the symptom spotting, the freaking out when  you wake up without symptoms one day, the constant impending doom feeling like you will miscarry any minute.

**[pbu21-0368]** — Reader context: Pregnancy glow narrative conflicts with actual skin changes.

> Why was i lied to? Who created this expectation of "baby glow"? And why is everyone glowing except me 😭. I was promised a glow and have yet to see any!!!

**[pbu32-0129]** — Reader context: Seven weeks pregnant after loss; heartbeat heard but peer posts revive fear.

> But I keep coming across "silent miscarriage" posts and then my head is spiralling what if since I don't have any symptoms what if I miscarried.

**[pbu32-0130]** — Reader context: Unconfirmed pregnancy and irregular cycles complicate app-based timing.

> i use the flo app and it isn’t always reliable in my case. (once a few months it may be accurate) my cycles usually last like 40-45 days instead of the common 21-35 days.

**[pbu32-0141]** — Reader context: Conflicting home measures create fear without previous miscarriage.

> I have an oura ring and it measures bbt every day & it’s been on a downward trend - still above baseline but not by much. My tests are progressively getting darker so I know hcg is all good but super worried about progesterone.

**[pbu32-0217]** — Reader context: Reassurance target moves from positive test to scan to heartbeat without ending anxiety.

> I waited for the heartbeat and now the tiktoks are in my head again. Having no symptoms is also not helping at all.

**[pbu32-0310]** — Reader context: Fifteen weeks with di/di twins; expected anxiety to ease after first trimester.

> I know I need to get off my phone, but I’m seeing so much miscarriage content and scary pregnancy things, especially surrounding twins.

### Additional evidence for pattern 4: A wanted, planned and supported pregnancy can still bring detachment, panic, anger and fear of never loving the baby.

**[pbu08-0006]** — Reader context: Fears SIDS following the death of a son.

> I’m honestly disappointed but not upset at the gender but I do feel extremely nervous about SIDS.

**[pbu08-0384]** — Reader context: New pregnancy a month after loss brings fear rather than happiness.

> And I’m terrified. I’m not happy I’m just scared, I’m scared it will happen again and I just don’t know what to feel or how to handle this.

**[pbu21-0217]** — Reader context: Wants happiness and connection during pregnancy.

> And I understand it’s the pregnancy hormones, but I’d like to be happy and enjoy the pregnancy and connect with my baby.

**[pbu21-0234]** — Reader context: Anxiety and anger rise while first appointment is still four weeks away.

> I am currently in my first trimester of my second pregnancy. I recently lost my first baby in December of 2025.  I recently found out I am pregnant again and the miscarriage anxiety has been awful. I feel as if when I get pregnant my emotions are a roller coaster, if I’m not anxious I’m furious. I constantly worry about the health of my baby. I am due at the OB in 4 weeks. Has anyone had similar experiences and have any tricks to help calm yourself?

**[pbu21-0385]** — Reader context: Diabetes diagnosis feels like failure despite trying to do everything right.

> I’ve spent this entire pregnancy trying so hard to do everything right for him. I already worry constantly about whether I’m doing enough, whether he’s okay, whether my body is doing what it’s supposed to do. Now finding out that my body isn’t processing blood sugar the way it should has made me feel like I’ve somehow let him down.

**[pbu21-0412]** — Reader context: Wants PTSD symptoms resolved before baby arrives.

> Of course I wanted a baby and a family of my own and I’m 32 now but I still feel like a giant kid myself sometimes. I struggle with panic attacks from ptsd so I just wish I was all better before the baby comes and doesn’t look like that can magically happen.

**[pbu32-0306]** — Reader context: Planned pregnancy with supportive marriage; nighttime sadness creates secondary guilt.

> I feel guilty for feeling this way, especially worrying that my baby might somehow feel my sadness too. Has anyone else experienced this during pregnancy?

### Additional evidence for pattern 5: Postpartum mental illness and intrusive thoughts can persist despite preparation, treatment or strong support.

**[pbu08-0127]** — Reader context: Recalls persistent contact-sleep needs beginning at five to six weeks and distressing thoughts during exhaustion.

> my baby girl is 4 months right now and when I first gave birth to her everything was fine up until about 5-6 weeks and then she’s been really clingy ever since so she doesn’t let me put her down, so she’s been contact napping/co-sleeping and I find myself having thoughts about hurting her when I get frustrated and overstimulated with her when I can’t sleep and she can’t either or can’t stop crying.

**[pbu08-0148]** — Reader context: Three-week hospital stay initially seems to explain distress, but it persists at home.

> I had a very complicated labour which led to an emergency c section, my daughter being in the NICU and a three week hospital stay for both of us. I really struggled mentally but thought it was because I missed home and wanted to be out. When we first got out, I made the most of having a newborn and loved being out and about and fitting her into my life.

**[pbu21-0093]** — Reader context: Newborn hospital admission intensifies guilt and constant monitoring.

> I was incredibly hysterical and crying. Like ugly crying and a complete mess. Looking back it made me realise I may have some kind of PTSD from my traumatic experience years ago. I still feel like I was negligent. I'm even more paranoid now. Everything from him sleeping to him feeding. How long, how many times, is he way too hot or way too cold, SIDS, etc. It's messing with me.

**[pbu32-0011]** — Reader context: Pregnancy feels unreal after miscarriage and ectopic pregnancy.

> It feels like I’m faking being pregnant. Even when I go to ultrasounds. I’m starting to show and I feel the baby kick all the time, but it doesn’t feel real!!

**[pbu32-0237]** — Reader context: After repeated early losses, stronger tests still do not feel emotionally real.

> I feel a total out of body experience, like I’m watching this happen to someone else and not to me.

### Additional evidence for pattern 6: Wanting motherhood, continuing this pregnancy, adoption and later trying again are separate decisions, with different forms of grief and relief.

**[pbu08-0008]** — Reader context: Seventeen-year-old wants a baby while recognizing an idealized view of parenthood.

> I am on birth control and have messed up (accidentally) while taking it the last two months and both times I have been scared, yet hopeful, that I’m pregnant. I am hyper-independent and overly mature due to upbringing, but understand that my brain is not fully developed. But what do I do if it’s constant wanting to get pregnant and have a baby? I have many people around me who would be happy to help and support my boyfriend and I, and I feel as if I have rose-coloured glasses on, because somehow I see no downsides, but can understand there are. I am just so confused. Please help.

**[pbu08-0084]** — Reader context: Friend reports an unexpected pregnancy after a partner claimed infertility.

> Come to find out he said he got an injury in Afghanistan that rendered him sterile and at the age of 34 this woman believed him. Now she’s pregnant. I told her if she planned on not keeping it she should go to a doctor to make sure she isnt too fat along. Suddenly she “knows for a fact” she cant be more than 3 or 4 weeks along. I feel like i am going insane. She said she hadnt slept with him for months and that she also was only 4 weeks along and didnt even realize that she contradicted herself in the same conversation.

**[pbu08-0105]** — Reader context: Wanted conception brings dread of newborn care and questions about future love.

> Sometimes I have fleeting feelings of love for my baby. But then other times I just dont feel excited at all and have seriously considered  abortion. I dont know what to do. 
> 
> A part of me just isn't ready and the thought of the new born stage terrifies me. I am not a maternal person at all. I dont know what to do with a newborn. 
> 
> Do you think my love will grow and I wont regret having this baby? Or do you think my intense dread and fear is something worth considering and that I should consider abortion?

**[pbu08-0114]** — Reader context: Positive test after partner's vasectomy raises dating and ectopic fears.

> Little back story- i know this is a positive test but im so confused about when and how my partner got a vasectomy a week after our daughter was born (she’s 7 months in 2 weeks) and i was due on the 20th of June im never late even straight after a baby and exclusively breastfeeding is now technically 12 days late but took these just to check fully expecting a negative. I’ve never had faints like this before and im very concerned about if baby will be healthy how far along i actually am especially as I’ve had an ectopic before.. I really don’t know how to feel about it which is definitely ok, but baby tried to get here clearly 😂 i just wonder if any one has any experience with this? X

**[pbu08-0184]** — Reader context: Possible pregnancy raises competing fears of abortion and harm from alcohol.

> I'm so scared of abortion and I think we would be able to take care of a child. But I'm also equally scared of if The child would have health problems, because of my drinking :(. 
> 
> I know that you are not medical professionals, but what amount of alcohol, and at what stage of pregnancy would be The most harmful?

**[pbu08-0202]** — Reader context: Partner avoids the promised discussion while the author feels attached and fears repeating abortion.

> I told him about 4 weeks in and now he’s telling me to get rid of it, basically having the complete opposite reaction as before. But then he told me he’s not completely against having it? My feelings are really just tossed all over the place and I feel so alone. He won’t talk to me about anything to do with the pregnancy, it’s like he’s avoiding it as much as he can like it will just go away. I’ve tried to talk to him about it multiples times and everytime he says “I just don’t think it’s a good idea. But if you really want to have it then we need to have a serious talk. ” And that’s basically where the conversation ends. No “serious” conversation ever happens, or any conversation at that. I don’t even think he knows how far along I am, he’s never asked. I told him I think I want to keep it because truthfully, that abortion was more painful mentally and physically than I thought it was going to be and I don’t know if I can handle going through it again and be okay. He’s the guy, he doesn’t have to go through the pain like I do and I don’t think he’s bothered to think about it. I feel so selfish saying that I want to keep it (especially in this economy) but I can’t explain the feeling I have about it. I feel attached to this baby i haven’t met yet.

**[pbu08-0225]** — Reader context: Possible conception arrives while a partner is grieving and has asked to postpone trying.

> But what if I am? I’m for abortion, but I don’t know if I would be willing to do it myself. How do I bring it up with him. Is he gonna be okay? Is he gonna fall apart completely? It’s so much on my mind I’d b so scared to tell him if it was true.

**[pbu08-0248]** — Reader context: Possible loss creates guilt about ambivalence toward an unexpected pregnancy.

> I know none of y'all will give me the answer. Im just sad because this whole pregnancy was a surprise and I had to decide if we keep it. If I lose this baby I will be sad it wasn't that "loved" as my first. I feel terrible because of that.

**[pbu08-0257]** — Reader context: Experienced foster parent fears pregnancy risks and lacks family help if something goes wrong.

> But here's the thing: I am afraid of pregnancy. I am afraid that something could go wrong, or that I could have a child with a disability or a medical condition that could bring them pain.
> 
> When I tell my close friends this, they often laugh and ask, "Why would you think, 'What if my baby comes with something?'" But I feel like that's a reasonable question to ask. What if?
> 
> Have you ever felt like that? I do not have close family, and one of my fears is: who would help us if something went wrong? What if something happened to me or my husband?
> 
> It's a strange thing to think about, but I find myself considering all the terrible yet possible scenarios.

**[pbu08-0297]** — Reader context: Loss creates fear of running out of time for a planned three-child family.

> Physically, I’ll recover, but emotionally I’m struggling with the fear that we’re running out of time. We’ve always dreamed of having three children, and now I’m worried that this miscarriage has set us back.  
> I’d love to hear from women who were around my age when they had a miscarriage.  
> Were you able to go on to have healthy pregnancies?  
> Were you still able to have two or three children after your loss?  
> Did your miscarriage affect your confidence about trying again?  
> Looking back now, did you feel like 31 was still young enough to build the family you wanted?

**[pbu08-0320]** — Reader context: Desire for a baby conflicts with timing and persists despite psychological support.

> But these feelings are stronger than I am. It feels like my heart and my mind are constantly fighting each other. Rationally, I know I should wait, but emotionally I feel an overwhelming sense of loss ever since the miscarriage.  
> I’m already seeing a psychologist and talking about it, so I do have professional support. Even so, these feelings haven’t gone away.

**[pbu08-0342]** — Reader context: Fears loss of personal life and relies on others' promise that feelings change after birth.

> I am pregnant and super fearful of losing my life. The only think I’m holding on to is everyone says something shifts when you have it and it all makes sense. I can’t stop thinking of sleepless nights, never being able to pay attention to anything but the baby, Elmo on repeat and my destroyed hot body

**[pbu08-0343]** — Reader context: Reconsiders planned termination while feeling guilty about substance exposure and online images.

> I am 9 weeks almost, I was going to have an abortion using the pills. I just don’t think I can do it. I’ve been smoking weed, and occasional vape the entire time. Have I already hurt the baby? Will they be okay if I decide not to take the pills? I have no clue what to do. I’m not in the best situation right now, but the images I saw online hurt to look at and made me feel ashamed.

**[pbu08-0348]** — Reader context: Grieves anticipated losses of identity and questions whether distress means she does not want the pregnancy.

> I’m crying all the time, i feel a grief for my life and everything i am going to have to say goodbye too. I feel like I am not going to be able to cope with being a mum and just the thought of anything regarding the baby is sending me into a spiral. I’ve felt so low I’ve considered an abortion which I know is awful but the idea of it brings me relief until I then realise that I wanted this and I do want a family (or at least I thought I did). My partner is very supportive but he thinks it’s all hormones and that I will be fine once the baby is here.

**[pbu08-0371]** — Reader context: Regrets earlier timing and communication while hoping the current pregnancy continues.

> I am so sad that I am in my late 30s. I will be an old mom. I am the youngest of my siblings and in-laws. By the time this kid is in their 4ps, we'll all be dead. I feel sad for them and angry with myself for not communicating better with my husband, angry with my husband for not communicating more with me, etc. 
> 
>   
> What do I do?? I'm filled with regret and sadness, along with shreds of hope and excitement about my current pregnancy.

**[pbu08-0374]** — Reader context: Unexpected conception brings dread of being unmedicated and a passive wish to die.

> That’s all out the window now. I don’t want to get an abortion because that was probably the worst part of my previous pregnancy. When my period was late for 3-4days I remember thinking - “*I hope it kills me this time.”* 
> 
> I am not ready to be completely unmediated and have to live with all the issues that come with it.

**[pbu08-0379]** — Reader context: Long-awaited pregnancy coincides with severe loss of motivation and frightening ambivalence.

> Over the last couple of days, though, my mental health has hit rock bottom. I can’t bring myself to do anything I normally enjoy, I don’t want to go to the gym or go running because I have no motivation, and every day feels like a blur. I’ve even had thoughts of not wanting this baby, which have scared me beyond belief, because I’ve wanted nothing more than to be pregnant for the last couple of years after waiting to be married and to have a stable home.

**[pbu21-0011]** — Reader context: Anxiety after IUD removal and ongoing spotting.

> My doctor didn’t seem concerned with the bleeding. She said the IUD needed to be removed because leaving it in could increase the risk of miscarriage, but she also explained that removing it can carry a risk as well. Since having it removed two weeks ago, I’ve been spotting almost everyday. I don’t see her again until next week, and it’s been causing me a lot of anxiety.

**[pbu21-0066]** — Reader context: Describes adoption advice and moderation in a pregnancy community.

> On a different sub, a pregnant teen was mad her parents wouldn’t let her abort (multiple reasons) and it’s illegal in her state. I said adoption could be a compromise because the baby lives AND you don’t have to raise it. I got permabanned for “Anti-choice rhetoric”.

**[pbu21-0083]** — Reader context: Family continues criticizing decision to keep baby one month after birth.

> her and my grandma keep telling me i made a mistake keeping my baby and that i should’ve focused on my studies and done better. i get where they’re coming from but the pregnancy was accidental and while i toyed with the idea of an abortion, i couldn’t do it. i just couldn’t

**[pbu21-0130]** — Reader context: Uncertain symptoms and negative test prompt another planned test.

> I wouldn't be totally upset if I were pregnant, but it wouldn't be ideal, all things considered. I'm going to test again in the next couple days to be safe if I still don't get my period but was curious if anyone else had something similar happen to them.

**[pbu21-0177]** — Reader context: Asks whether faint tests and unusual bleeding meant pregnancy loss.

> was 7 days late to my period which i never am late usually early if anything, my periods are light and don’t last very long. during the whole week i took a pregnancy test and it showed very very faint in a pink dye test, while waiting for my period i had tender nipple and breasts, headaches (worse than the usual), very gassy and needed to per a lot. come when it started it started hard very clotty which never usually is and just a lot, i filled tampons quicker than normal. also within the last month and a half ish i stoped taking my birth control OPill, but following up to that i was very inconsistent with taking it. would this be a chemical pregnancy?

**[pbu21-0186]** — Reader context: Wanted unexpected pregnancy brings fear around blood results and family loss history.

> I’m 19, I just found out I’m pregnant, hcg 459 and progesterone is 4.2. I know I’m young and pregnancy or a baby is not planned, but I want this baby. I’m so scared right now. I am so so scared right now. My mother had multiple miscarriages, and I think it is the most painful thing in the world to find out I’m pregnant, just to find out I’m miscarrying. How do I calm down?

**[pbu21-0192]** — Reader context: At four months postpartum fears scar problems or an unrecognized new pregnancy.

> I am 4m pp, from a c-section. all of a sudden today, I have some very light spotting-pink and brown. My abdomen hurts too and just feel kinda crumby. I had my period about 2 weeks ago so not that. Im really nervous about some issue with the csection or scar ect I also have irrational fears of somehow being pregnant again or even miscarriage if thats the case but it does hurt.

**[pbu21-0201]** — Reader context: After multiple losses, desired pregnancy collides with changed finances.

> Up until the last couple of days I really let my guard down and “let myself “ be excited. I didn’t guard my heart, even though I know I have every good reason to. I’ve had two scans and everything looks perfect. But… even though everything in my bones wants to have this baby… I’m hesitant and just keep thinking that this couldn’t be a worse time. Which I feel horrible for. I’ve thought that maybe terminating would be best. My mind is everywhere and I’m terrified each direction I look.

**[pbu21-0223]** — Reader context: Prior traumatic abortion and loved ones’ excitement complicate current decision.

> i’ve already previously terminated a pregnancy last year and that’s most of the reason why is the guilt. also my partner wants it and is very happy and looking forward to us being a family and my mom is really excited as well. my abortion (MA) experience was extremely traumatic and painful and i ended up having to be hospitalized, and in all honesty i know it sound stupid because i know that childbirth is painful as well, but i just really don’t wanna put myself through that pain again it was severely traumatizing for me. so there’s just a mix of a lot of emotions right now but guilt more than anything of my even giving my baby a chance.

**[pbu21-0229]** — Reader context: After abortion during planned surgery, asks whether viability explanations softened the facts.

> I was scheduled for laparoscopic endometriosis excision surgery and iud insertion this morning. two days ago I had 4 positive pregnancy tests (I had also taken one four days ago which was negative at that point). I was 5w0d today. They offered to do a d&c (abortion) during the surgery. They tried to find it on transabdominal and vaginal US before we went to the OR but could not visualize it. My HCG level was 81. my surgeon implied that it was low and basically likely to miscarry. but was she sugar coating it? did I have any chance of the pregnancy progressing? Or was it completely non viable?? I was really torn up about the decision and grieving a lot. So knowing that it was likely going to miscarry/not be viable gives me some comfort that it wasn’t the right time. If you have any insight please let me know :)

**[pbu21-0259]** — Reader context: IUD pregnancy creates simultaneous uncertainty about viability and desired timing.

> I have so many questions and feel like so much is unknown. Through my research I feel like I should be having way more bleeding if I’m miscarrying but this would be my first so not sure. My husband and I will  have to make a decision possibly on Tuesday if it is viable as we were wanting to wait 2-3 more years and we’re only wanting 1 (if any). Still in so much shock that I’m even pregnant 😭 I haven’t told anyone in my family I’m a very independent person. But I had to share and see if anyone had experienced anything similar.

**[pbu21-0278]** — Reader context: Graduation hopes and fear of future infertility complicate termination decision.

> Also, I am also close to graduating from college, and have so many goals.. I feel like I was just finally getting myself to a safe space where I loved myself and chose me. Now I’m stuck making such a massive decision. I’m so afraid I will regret this for the rest of my life. What if I can’t get pregnant again? What if this was my answered prayer from long, long ago? When I think of it logically, it doesn’t make sense to have a child with a volatile man, when I’m so close to graduating and achieving my goals. But when I listen to my heart, I imagine it all working out somehow.

**[pbu21-0289]** — Reader context: Parents’ enthusiasm changes how she views termination.

> Despite all that, my parents were excited and immediately supportive when I told them. They don’t know who the father is. Their reaction made me question my decision to terminate because I’ve always imagined becoming a mom around this age, and part of me feels like having a child could fill a sense of family I’ve never had.

**[pbu21-0294]** — Reader context: Discovers pregnancy after usual baths, medical cannabis and vaping.

> Now I have the best reason to! I’m 7 weeks pregnant and just found out. But I need to know, have I done horrible damage so far from smoking and taking hot baths daily? I feel like I have the most unclean oven to cook this bun in and I’m so scared.

**[pbu21-0317]** — Reader context: Unexpected pregnancy loss creates grief without prior intention to conceive.

> It’s not my first child and I wasn’t expecting this but I told myself I could be ok with the idea of another addition to our family. It’s been a long week to say the least. I am devastated and just wish I could go on a month long vacation. How do you get over the grief of someone who didn’t even get a chance to exist nevertheless someone you weren’t even trying to create? Also this all happened h the week before my birthday 🙃. I can’t even make this shit up. Life has been lifing real bad.  Everything is stressing me out. I’m on edge and I just wish I could lay in the same spot forever. Sometimes I cry. Sometimes I say nothing. I know it’s still early and I don’t expect it to be all figured out but just needing to vent and hear others experiences, which helps me a lot.

**[pbu21-0363]** — Reader context: Disability becomes family’s argument against parenting and for abortion.

> Then we found out I was pregnant. We told her the same day because we were scared and didn’t know what to do. She told me that I can’t even tie my own shoes so how would I take care of a baby. Also called us idiots but mixed in some nicer sounding things like mistakes happen. She said she wasn’t gonna be a baby sitter so don’t expect anything out of her. Which I wasn’t in the first place. She then suggested I get an abortion. Even though she doesn’t support abortion. That one bothered me a lot. I’m pro choice but I didn’t want to get an abortion. We told her we’re keeping it and she didn’t seem happy but accepted it and went back to whatever she was doing.

**[pbu21-0375]** — Reader context: IUD risk prompts an urgent next-day appointment after positive tests.

> I know the risk of an ectopic pregnancy with an IUD, so I called my obgyn and I’m going in first thing tomorrow (Monday, it’s Sunday today) for ultrasound and blood work.

**[pbu21-0392]** — Reader context: Believes parenting is manageable but pregnancy and birth may not be.

> Anyway, this choice is not nearly as easy or straightforward as I had imagined. I really don't want to abort *our* baby. But already the idea and experience of being pregnant is overwhelming and the thought of giving birth freaks me tf out. I also can't imagine going through with this whole experience just to give the kid up. I genuinely think we could handle raising a kid well and its not the idea of parenting that I have a hard time with. I oscillate between feeling like I'm okay to just see how this goes (its obviously still early on and lots of things could happen) and being absolutely shaken to my core about everything I'm already experiencing and how it's only going to get worse physically.

**[pbu21-0405]** — Reader context: Wants motherhood while feeling everyone except partner rejects the choice.

> I’ve always wanted to be a mother. I want to experience it. I want to create a life and give it the best possible chance at succeeding in joy, in hopes that maybe they will live out something fulfilling. Idk, I just got out of an abusive relationship a bit ago, and this man is promising me everything I want. And I think I know deep in my gut I really want this. I guess I just feel like. Wrong? For wanting it? Like everyone and everything around me is telling me I should just get rid of it.

**[pbu32-0043]** — Reader context: Disagreement over continuing pregnancy despite savings, family support and available leave.

> I offered another compromise of having the abortion if he agrees we can try for another baby, aiming to have it when I’m 23. He was super against this and emphasised how he wants me to have the abortion and “try when we both feel we’re ready” which I feel will mean he puts it off until we’re older than I’d like.

**[pbu32-0050]** — Reader context: Possible pregnancy complicates breakup and parenting arrangements for existing toddler.

> There was also a chance of me being pregnant, he kept referring to it as a clump of cells, and emphasized how much he didn’t want it. So I told him that if I am, likely I’d be keeping it.

**[pbu32-0051]** — Reader context: Husband's reaction to intentional second pregnancy undermines author's happiness.

> He became so negative about it that I started spiraling. I couldn’t even be happy about it myself at that point. All I could think of was I ruined everything. I got so low that I offered to him the option of termination.

**[pbu32-0113]** — Reader context: Prospective uncle's disapproval conflicts with desire to support pregnancy.

> my main concern is the fact i don’t know how to interact with him or his baby momma (we white not gonna lie) and the first time they told me I was immediately aiming for the abortion button ngl. Which is crazy cuz i been heavily against abortion till that moment (i get it now). But since they ain’t going that route, and to keep this story short ig, how tf am i supposed to act?

**[pbu32-0120]** — Reader context: Eighteen-year-old with unconfirmed pregnancy describes shame, longstanding pregnancy fear and suicidal thoughts.

> Every day leading up to my period has been immense dread, and now that it's not here yet, I just feel like such a useless, unwanted, disgusting carrier for this parasite I know will kill me. I can't. Be pregnant.

**[pbu32-0125]** — Reader context: Unexpected pregnancy despite implant and prior fertility advice; partner presses abortion or adoption.

> Am I a horrible person for wanting to keep this baby? He’s made it clear that he doesn’t want the child at all but I just mentally don’t think that I could handle the abortion or adoption route.

**[pbu32-0153]** — Reader context: Unplanned pregnancy in seven-month relationship; supportive partner does not remove dread.

> What I fear almost most of all is how it will affect our relationship. I waited so long to find a relationship so wonderful and I can’t wrap my head around how it’s going to change.

**[pbu32-0166]** — Reader context: Conflicting screening and scan findings create tension between testing risks and delayed decisions.

> We don’t want to do anything to harm our baby since we were already high risk for preterm labor due to the hematoma. I’ve read that amnio can potentially cause a miscarriage. However, we also don’t want to wait until 28 weeks to find out our baby has trisomy 21 or other issues and then have to potentially  make super tough decision very late in the pregnancy.

**[pbu32-0187]** — Reader context: Wanted pregnancy and severe nausea conflict with planned move abroad.

> My mind goes back and forth constantly. One moment I’m excited about the baby, the next I’m grieving the life I thought I was about to have. Then I feel guilty for even thinking that way after experiencing two miscarriages.

**[pbu32-0196]** — Reader context: Unexpected pregnancy after being told conception would be nearly impossible.

> I am heavily considering abortion but I just cant find the will to continue with it. My partner said he would support me in any way he could but he told me when we found out on Saturday that he truly didnt want anymore kids

**[pbu32-0202]** — Reader context: Planned pregnancy happens faster than expected; feelings shift from fear of loss to relief at possibility.

> I just dont feel like i want my life to change, and i certainly dont want to be pregnant. I have an eating disorder, and have no idea how im going to cope with my changing body. Id also say ive got a drinking problem and  im struggling so much with all the restrictions and rules.

**[pbu32-0207]** — Reader context: Partner initially proposed trying but became indifferent after conception.

> I’m scared that after the baby is born and he decides he does not want this life, he will leave, and I’ll be a single mother.

**[pbu32-0223]** — Reader context: Delays telling religious parents until screening because they oppose any termination.

> However, I am mourning not being able to share this pregnancy with who is supposed to be one of our support systems.

**[pbu32-0245]** — Reader context: Unexpected pregnancy amid spinal pain, new relationship and prior agreement not to have children.

> I just don’t know what to do and I’m scared of making a decision that I’m going to regret or that will harm our relationship.

**[pbu32-0276]** — Reader context: Prior agreement changes emotionally after positive test; partner still wants termination.

> We’ve had previous conversations about abortion if we were to get pregnant right now, but I didn’t realize I would feel so different at finding out.

**[pbu32-0284]** — Reader context: Stable finances and supportive partner do not settle fear of missing twenties.

> I keep reading and hearing that I’ll feel the love and excitement when it’s here what if I still feel regret about my lost years??

**[pbu32-0289]** — Reader context: Planned pregnancy; longstanding Lexapro no longer feels effective.

> The depression is crippling. I’m hardly eating, only wanna sleep and when I’m awake, I’ll stare at the ceiling. It’s to the point where I’ve looked into getting an abortion.

**[pbu32-0293]** — Reader context: Both feel unable to parent but disagree over adoption versus abortion.

> My boyfriend doesn’t want to do adoption because he feels guilty and he thinks he would feel he has to end up keeping the baby because he thinks it’s giving the baby up and that would give him a lot of guilt which I understand but I feel the same way about abortion that I’m giving up on the baby’s chance of a life.

**[pbu32-0308]** — Reader context: After loss, wonders whether first-trimester aversion to parenting predicts lasting preference.

> Now it’s been a while and I’m just so conflicted. Was it the first tri hormones? Should I try again? Was it just reality hitting me in the face?

**[pbu32-0322]** — Reader context: Orders abortion pills after partner's budget calculation and stated unreadiness; wants emotional acknowledgment.

> Im upset that rather than saying “we can get ready” he said “we’re not ready”

**[pbu32-0329]** — Reader context: Unconfirmed pregnancy; author describes suicidal fear of remaining pregnant or giving birth.

> I don't see a future where me and this clump of cells lives. Either I die during pregnancy, during birth, or I just end it before that happens.
