**[pbu08-0048]** — Reader context: Parents provide practical help, but the husband asks that they stop visiting for a month.

> he keeps pointing out how most husbands don’t cook. My parents have actually been coming over every weekend to help me with household chores because they know we’ve fought over it before. 3rd trimester has been more manageable than my 1st or 2nd so I have been trying my best to finish doing laundry before they arrive as they don’t listen to me when I tell them to stop doing my chores. They are stubborn but I really am grateful for them as they are a huge help. However, my husband recently expressed annoyance about how he sees my parents more than he sees his in a year. He has asked me to not let my parents come over for a month

**[pbu08-0054]** — Reader context: Friend describes a pregnant divorcee feeling unreal and having no time to research parenthood.

> She admitted that she has been keeping busy because when she’s still she has to think about things, and that it’s been stressful and she hasn’t had any time to research anything about parenthood, parenting, or lifespan development (I asked if she had read any books or anything and she said no). She let me know that it doesn’t feel real to her and it freaks her out, and of course she’s dealing with a lot of guilt about raising her son without his father. I told her she has NO reason to feel guilty about this!!

**[pbu08-0082]** — Reader context: Partner withdraws before birth and cheats four weeks postpartum.

> In the beginning he was amazing, so supportive, helpful, and made me feel so loved. But right before our son was born, he completely checked out.
> 
> At 4 weeks postpartum, he cheated on me with his ex and got her pregnant. Around the same time, I realized he’s a full blown alcoholic who drinks almost every single day. I’ve been clawing at the walls trying to fix this, begging him to work on himself and our family, and trying to save our family at the same time, but I’m just so exhausted at this point. I’m the only one trying.

**[pbu08-0083]** — Reader context: After an abortion, partner asks to make her pregnant again.

> To make things even worse, I had an abortion just 2 months ago because our relationship and life were already such a mess. It was incredibly painful, but now he’s turning around and saying he wants to get me pregnant again. The mind games are just insane.

**[pbu08-0166]** — Reader context: Conflicting conception dates affect paternity fears and a decision about continuing pregnancy.

> During my ultrasound the doctor stated based on her findings conception happened on Dec 13 and that Dec 22 or later was too late. 
> 
> I would have had an abortion if there were any chance it was the abusive partners. It is not a safe situation. 
> 
> My 11 week ultrasound and OB doctor is telling me conception happened around the 24th. Does anyone have any insight into this? I would greatly appreciate it 🙏

**[pbu08-0283]** — Reader context: New partner responds gently to a pregnancy anxiety trigger from previous cheating.

> I felt really silly and just started crying, he was so lovely and just reassured me. I mean, his phone doesn't even have a password on it and he said he understands and I can look any time I want (I don't want to go down that route though).

**[pbu08-0378]** — Reader context: Former partner pressures abortion and uses existing body-image vulnerability in frightening comments.

> As soon as he got here, he asked if I would get an abortion. I said I was still in shock, and that I did not know yet, and that we should speak through options. Instantly he told me he would want nothing to do with me or "it", and instantly went below the belt to tell me that the pregnancy will ruin my body (I have anorexia, so this was a low blow) and that my life will be over. He started acting very scary, so I comforted him (I know, I'm pathetic) and we spoke no more of it.

**[pbu08-0399]** — Reader context: After separation and temporary homelessness, feels relieved, happy and able to enjoy pregnancy.

> The mental peace I have from that. Transcends everything that has happened. I literally walk around happy and just talk to my baby in there, I think I can feel movement at 13 weeks. I wake up from a nap or just randomly start smiling because I’m happy. I always wanted to be a mom, and I always wanted one child. I see my bd like a sperm donor.

**[pbu08-0400]** — Reader context: Remains in an unwanted relationship to retain anticipated hands-on newborn help.

> However, having to still pretend to want to be with him, haa been so exhausting and has made my pregnancy probably a bit more stressful than it needed to be. I just absolutely do not want to the newborn early months by myself. And I just know if I left while pregnant he would try to make things as inconvenient as possible for me.

**[pbu21-0024]** — Reader context: Friends push adoption while the author wants to keep the pregnancy.

> My friends are pro life and they keep pushing me to give the baby up for adoption. Adoption is beautiful, please don’t get me wrong. But both of my daughters will grow up having a sibling they won’t know their entire life. Both of our families know about the pregnancy and are happy about it. I would be taking my child away from a family that already loves them. To me personally, if I chose adoption I would feel like a handmaid and I would always regret it. I want to believe that I am strong enough to fix this shit myself. But my friends keep throwing families that want kids in my face. I know they mean well, but it makes me feel like they think I am incapable. Idk. I’m being sensitive and I just need to figure out my shit and fast

**[pbu21-0045]** — Reader context: Wants recovery at home and worries about stairs at the mother-in-law’s apartment.

> He also said earlier that we should come here when the baby is born instead of go home and i literally just want to go home with our baby and be able to heal and become a mother in private not in his moms apartment (which is on the second floor so i would have to go up 2 flights of stairs just to get in here while being freshly postpartum, i’m already struggling with the stairs here due to extreme pelvic pain as it is.) I just want to feel supported and cared for and he doesn’t most of the time.

**[pbu21-0174]** — Reader context: Partner interprets an early uncertain scan as proof there is no baby.

> My boyfriend has made it very clear that he doesn’t want this baby. He hasn’t really considered my feelings in all of this ,  his main concern is himself, how this affects him, his image, his life. And it’s made me realize a lot about our relationship… that maybe I love him a lot more than he loves me. He clearly does love me in some ways but he doesn't understand what I'm feeling and I don't really expect him to . When I went for my first scans the doc said she couldn't find a heartbeat which is completely normal in early pregnancy and he took it as it's dead , there is no baby and there's nothing actually there .

**[pbu21-0210]** — Reader context: Partner criticizes appearance during late pregnancy.

> He told me today that he thinks my face is pretty but me being fat is not his favorite but there's ways we can work around that. Im just so confused, does he love me for me if I have to change everything? I was fat before I was pregnant too, if anything I've lost a little weight being pregnant.

**[pbu21-0215]** — Reader context: Argument escalates to threatened eviction four days after learning of pregnancy.

> I left the house to get some fresh air. He’s just asked me to leave entirely, and threw my clothes out of the closet. I told him to give me some grace and be patient, but he asked me how patient can someone be. I don’t know what to do. I have no one else here.

**[pbu21-0244]** — Reader context: Early relationship included feeling unsafe and begging to go home.

> Very early on, there were incidents involving alcohol, extreme jealousy, accusations that I was being unfaithful, emotional outbursts, and situations where I felt genuinely unsafe and afraid. There were moments where I was begging for space, begging to go home, and wondering how things had escalated so quickly.

**[pbu21-0245]** — Reader context: Recognizes self-neglect while trying to understand partner.

> Lately I feel like I’ve spent so much energy understanding him that I’ve stopped listening to myself.

**[pbu21-0246]** — Reader context: Threats and removal of ultrasound photos crystallize fear with nowhere to go.

> This is my first pregnancy and I’m scared that this stress I am going through with my partner is affecting my baby , my partner emotionally abuses me and threatens to hit me … he tells me to leave and to take the baby he doesn’t want nothing to do with me or him , we have been fighting a lot but today drew the line when he took our baby’s ultrasound pictures off the wall , have you experienced emotional abuse during your pregnancy and what did you do ? I have nowhere to go or family to rely on I have two dogs I go to school full time and just got hired on at a job today I am just waiting to get my start date.

**[pbu21-0277]** — Reader context: Describes threats of full custody and refusal to accept co-parenting.

> He is a nasty, mean drunk, verbally abused me, and has threatened to fight for full custody once I give birth because he says I have no option but to have a family with him, as he will not settle for co-parenting arrangements. Also, continues to contact me nonstop making all my appointments about him & how I’ve ruined fatherhood for him.

**[pbu21-0306]** — Reader context: Reports inconsistent scan explanations and abortion discussion directed to husband.

> She said let’s do transvaginal. Said she found the sacs, baby but no heartbeat. she said what she found earlier wasn’t the heartbeat but the yolk sac noise and that it makes noise. While I was empty my bladder, she was convincing my husband to do an abortion and asked him if im on medications (he clarified I’ve only been taking my prenatals (Nordic brand) and no other medications) because I’m measuring at 6 weeks, mind you this is before the transvaginal.

**[pbu21-0332]** — Reader context: Sister’s threat to find future child shapes desire for pregnancy privacy.

> I asked my sister if someone abuses you over and over would you keep forgiving them and go back to them? Immediately she said YES OF COURSE and im like dude that's not okay and try to explain to her how I would never let my kids around that if I could help it. She then said something along the lines of "what when you have kids are you just not going to tell them they have an aunt?" And then she followed it up with something along the lines of "ill come find you and the kid and MAKE SURE they know" it absolutely scared me.

**[pbu32-0055]** — Reader context: Third pregnancy amid no outside help and very long partner workdays.

> Husband told me this morning at 6 am, the moment I opened my eyes that he wants an abortion. This is after I asked if I could sleep in an hour because I sleep with both boys and am up all night while he sleeps on a couch.

**[pbu32-0072]** — Reader context: Recalls partner's aggression during pregnancy; current child is eighteen months.

>

I brought that up to my dad. And what pisses me off the most is that he and my mom had such trouble conceiving. They had so many losses before they got me (my dad is my brothers' step dad). I would've thought if anyone understood, it would be him and my mom.

**[pbu08-0412]** — Reader context: Pain recalls a previous ectopic pregnancy and prompts frantic searching while awaiting an appointment.

It’s like we went from the highest of highs to the lowest of lows in a span of 5-6 days.

**[pbu21-0006]** — Reader context: Partner describes testing, symptom checking and fear of another loss.

> She's been sitting at home and watching reels about pregnancy, constantly evaluating if such and such symptom is a good sign or a bad sign, if a lack of X symptom is good or bad, etc. She doesn't want to be nauseous because it's unpleasant, but she doesn't want to not be nauseous because, "Aren't I supposed to be nauseous right now?? Is something wrong???"

**[pbu21-0041]** — Reader context: Prior loss leaves partner opposing long travel despite reported medical clearance.

> My husband, however, is not on board with the idea of a long-haul flight. He is incredibly supportive and takes amazing care of me, but I feel he is being overcautious because of our past loss. His main concerns are:  
> The extreme distance and flight duration.  
> The jet lag disrupting my sleep cycle.  
> The drastic weather change.  
> I really want to see my family, but I also want to respect his feelings.  
> Have any of you done a similar long-haul trip during your second trimester? How did it go? More importantly, how can I help ease my husband's anxiety, or should I reconsider the trip?

**[pbu21-0121]** — Reader context: Partner tries to reassure about faint test lines.

> She has since had a period and we started trying again and have had a very feint positive and my wife is very anxious about the whole thing, her next period is due early next week so I know its an early time to test, she's worried about the line not getting any darker and I've tried explaining that it's still very early days yet and that a line is a line.

**[pbu21-0122]** — Reader context: Balances personal excitement with reassuring wife despite uncertain outcome.

> I'm struggling with both trying not to get too excited but also reassuring my wife that things are going to be okay. (even if this one doesn't take).

**[pbu21-0153]** — Reader context: Naming disagreement began shortly after birth and reflects family estrangement.

> My wife wants to change our son's last name because she doesn't want him sharing a name with this man. This did cause some fights between us shortly after he was born, but at this point I'm mostly in agreement with her. Now the conflict is an internal one within myself instead of being a conflict between me and her.

**[pbu21-0162]** — Reader context: Approaching birth brings envy of friends with available grandparents.

> As the due date approaches, we're finding ourselves talking ourselves out of the envy we have been feeling towards all the people around us that seem to take for granted how lucky they are to have their parents to guide them, or at least to take some load off.

**[pbu21-0305]** — Reader context: Wants realistic reassurance during wait after bleeding.

> We’re trying not to get our hopes up too much before the ultrasound, but we’re also looking for some realistic reassurance.  
> Has anyone experienced:  
> Heavy or moderate bleeding around 8–9 weeks,  
> Dark/old blood,  
> High hCG levels,  
> No cramping or severe pain,  
> …and then gone on to have a healthy pregnancy?  
> I know no one can tell us what tomorrow’s ultrasound will show, but hearing real experiences while we wait would mean a lot. Thank you.

**[pbu32-0071]** — Reader context: One week after emergency section, normal tests do not resolve partner's birth questions.

> My only concern is that I am wondering if him not crying, being stunned, and needing a bit of assistance with breathing during birth is a big deal and something I should be concerned about?

**[pbu32-0117]** — Reader context: After four miscarriages, new baby's arrival has not softened grandparent's criticism.

> I had hoped that the birth of her first biological Grandchild might help to mellow her out but the truth is that she's brought my wife and I to tears twice in less than 3 months with her criticism of our parenting.

**[pbu32-0194]** — Reader context: Sudden fear of generic Tylenol leaves author in pain despite therapist and doctor involvement.

> I'll get the medicine out, put it in my hand and then put it back in the bottle. My husband held my hand while I was trying to take it and stayed up with me. I never took it and he needed to go to bed.

**[pbu32-0195]** — Reader context: Follow-up: takes medication after peer support and receives partner encouragement.

> My husband woke up temporarily and asked if I took it, I said yes. He kissed me on the forehead and said, "I'm so proud of you" 🥲

### Additional evidence for pattern 9: Housework, food, sleep, newborn care and emotional support can remain with one parent even when the partner is present.

**[pbu08-0215]** — Reader context: Partner's preconception promises of pregnancy care do not match the experience.

> Before I got pregnant we would have looooong convos about how we want to parent, how we’d want to deal with certain things, the works. He’d constantly tell me how i’d be well taken care of and treated during pregnancy and, well obviously he hasn’t.

**[pbu08-0284]** — Reader context: Approaching birth revives fears about how pregnancy can change a relationship.

> You are right and it's these pregnancy hormones, I was getting in a downwards spiral earlier. I think getting closer to the finish line as well and anxiety about how that can affect relationships. Having found out years later that my ex had cheated on me throughout the pregnancy and stuff has done a number on me, but I'm not going to let that defeat me! Just need to get out of my head.

**[pbu21-0204]** — Reader context: Partner privately rejects fatherhood after public preparation and enthusiasm.

> 2 weeks ago I found out that he had been telling his friends and family he doesn’t want to be a dad after months of telling me he did. He came to every appointment, was there for both ultrasounds, posted about our son and told everyone how excited he was for his baby boy.

**[pbu21-0226]** — Reader context: Friend reports partner dismissing tears during a threatened pregnancy.

> P.s. I'm sure her husband helps and suppory her, but she just told me he was annoyed that she kept crying last Monday. Per her words, he said "can you stop crying already? Stop acting like a baby, you're gonna be a mother soon". I wanted to slap him, unfortunately he wasn't home when I visited her today.

**[pbu21-0254]** — Reader context: Joy after positive test coexists with constant concern following suspected loss.

> My husband(28) and I(24) found out we’re expecting on Friday, and the lady at my OB’s office said I was most likely around four weeks along. It’s the first for both of us and we’re over the moon! We had what we suspect to have been a miscarriage in March ‘25 and naturally we’ve both been super on edge and concerned about every little thing, as we REALLY want a child. I guess the point of this is to ask for literally any advice from other moms out there to soothe this anxious FTM’s heart.

**[pbu32-0140]** — Reader context: Young pregnant author feels guilty accepting willing support from boyfriend's family.

> I feel like if they help me out with childcare or have me live with them I’ll be taking advantage of them. He keeps trying to reassure me, but I just don’t want to be a nuisance.

**[pbu32-0203]** — Reader context: Physically easy pregnancy after loss; partner and colleague criticize irritability.

> One even said “your only pregnancy symptom is being an asshole” - I agree I’ve been a bit irritable but I don’t feel I’ve been so crazy horrible that I deserve this.

### Additional evidence for pattern 10: Disclosure and relatives can bring support, judgement, unwanted exposure or isolation, especially after loss.

**[pbu08-0003]** — Reader context: After two losses, questions parenthood and lacks someone with a similar experience.

> After that I put everything on pause and felt like maybe I wasn’t meant to have kids. Now over a year later I’m still struggling with the thought of it but also recognizing that I’m getting older and my chances will get slimmer. I am still confused on if kids are right for us. I was so excited when I thought I had gotten pregnant again, bawling my eyes out, thanking God. After I found out it was another loss I was confused, crushed, and pissed off. Anyone out there go through this and end up deciding to have kids or not? Also I know it’s a preference and ultimately my decision. I just don’t really have anyone I know personally who can relate.

**[pbu08-0036]** — Reader context: Worries her complications prevent a relative from enjoying a healthy pregnancy.

> I’m worried all my troubles are taking away from my SIL’s enjoyment of her pregnancy. I keep wanting to send her a message to say that she should enjoy hers guilt free (she has a tendency to feel very deeply for others) but I’m worried that might sound trite. Should I say anything? My husband (her brother) has spoken to her but I feel that maybe I should say something too. We live in different countries so don’t see each other much.

**[pbu08-0070]** — Reader context: Pregnancy exposes unresolved family wounds and unmet expectations of support.

> Around sixteen weeks, things started to feel heavier. Old family trauma resurfaced, and I began to see certain people more clearly than I had before. I had always heard that having a baby brings families closer together, but for me, it started to feel like the opposite. There were wounds in my own family that had never truly healed, only been brushed over with time. There had never been a real apology, just years passing by and everyone pretending that was enough. My mother’s negativity began weighing on me more and more, and her lack of support while I tried to plan my baby shower became hard to ignore. I have never felt like a bigger burden to anyone.

**[pbu08-0132]** — Reader context: Family dismisses distress, surgery and need to rest as weakness or laziness.

> His family has not been supportive. My inlaws were in town but refused to help. My MIL called me dramatic during pregnancy when I was bedridden from severe vomiting, and after the birth sent messages saying postpartum depression is a “weak mind” issue and that I was exaggerating how hard labour was. My FIL laughed at me and told me my emergency C-section was “the easy way out.”
> 
> My own mum came to “help” for a few days not long after the birth but did very little. She complained when I cried or needed help. I could barely move or stand after surgery, she insisted I do nappy changes and said I was being lazy and not bonding with my baby. I was still in shock from the birth struggling with my recovery. I felt I wasn’t allowed to rest properly.

**[pbu08-0137]** — Reader context: Recalls practical newborn questions answered with advice to soak up the moments.

> I asked months ago about how to get anything done with a newborn and the replies were all basically “just soak in these moments mama” which doesn’t help at all. And it hasn’t gotten better.

**[pbu08-0140]** — Reader context: Friend's infertility makes the author's difficult newborn experience feel invalidated.

> When she checked in on me newly postpartum I told her about my experience to which she responded with “I wish I had a baby to wake up to every couple hours and feed”.  She also said “I grew up taking care of my siblings so I already have baby experience.” I was aghast when I first heard that, because it felt so invalidating but I let it slide cause I know that I probably would’ve felt the same way in her shoes.

**[pbu08-0164]** — Reader context: Balances hiding pregnancy with avoiding attention at a family birthday.

> We’re trying to decide if it would be best to tell them at the very start of the week before the celebration part or TRY and get by on excuses and tell them at the end of the week? 
> 
> I just don’t want the pregnancy to overshadow his celebrations or be one of those people who tries to steal all the attention in the room. I actively hate having a lot of attention to be honest.

**[pbu08-0165]** — Reader context: Unauthorized announcement feels like losing a special pregnancy moment.

> I really have no idea what to do now. It feels like something so special and important to me was taken away from me. I can’t stop crying, I’m still new to this but it feels like the stress is making me physically ill. My dad has been great and said to cut her out for now and that he’s really not surprised. I guess I was too hopeful.

**[pbu08-0191]** — Reader context: Peer reassurance helps reduce overanalysis of bodily changes.

> Ugh thank you so much! I’m trying very hard not to overly pick apart my body and what I’m feeling. I know my body is going through changes and things are going to feel different!

**[pbu08-0210]** — Reader context: Hopeful after two losses, seeks both healthy outcomes and ways to handle worry.

> I got my positive test yesterday and I am seriously over the moon… I have  good feeling things will be okay, I keep reassuring myself and keeping so much hope and happiness in front of me
> 
> Of course, there’s still anxiety.   
> Anything can happen and I know from experience… 
> 
> I would like to read some experiences from others going through similar situations and going on to delivering a healthy baby
> 
> How did you deal with the worry? Did you do anything different?

**[pbu08-0239]** — Reader context: Sets newborn contact boundaries and faces a grandfather's claim of entitlement.

> I was sleeping and didnt see his text but I had told him I didnt want him around my child when shes born. Im 30 weeks pregnant tomorrow and im getting to the point in life where im cutting certain family out of my life permanently. He told me he was entitled to see her. I said being blood related doesn't give him any right to see my daughter.

**[pbu08-0253]** — Reader context: Sister-in-law dismisses mobility-limiting sciatica to demand a gender reveal.

> I told his sister I was close to cancelling the gender reveal due to sciatica pain and couldn’t walk and she said to me “well you’re gonna have to start walking soon, I want to know the gender”.

**[pbu08-0254]** — Reader context: Relatives disregard privacy requests during possible ectopic complications.

> I feel like I just don’t want them around my child. I might be over reacting but during my first trimester I asked them to keep the pregnancy to themselves because I had complications and was in hospital cos they thought it was going to be ectopic but they ended up telling EVERYONE after we told them more than ten times not to tell any more people.

**[pbu08-0273]** — Reader context: Suspected recurrent loss prompts a request for solidarity and hope.

> I guess what I want to hear, Im not alone?? Im perfectly healthy, active 31y women, I never thought this will be experience… give me hope Ill get my baby at the end? I just keep crying now.

**[pbu08-0290]** — Reader context: After previously telling people about a loss, still wants to share an exciting early pregnancy.

> I’ve told people in the past both times at 4 weeks pregnant. I know it’s ridiculously early but I can’t keep secrets and it’s too exciting of news for me to not want to share. The second time I had a miscarriage at 7 weeks and had to tell everyone, wasn’t fun but I came to terms with it by the time I told most people. I’m pregnant again 4 weeks now and I know I shouldn’t say anything and I most likely won’t, but it’s killing me. My in laws know because I live with them but I want to tell other people.

**[pbu08-0308]** — Reader context: Crosspost adds support from the husband's family amid disappointment in her own.

> What has helped is my husband’s family. They have been supportive in the ways I hoped my own family would be, and I am so grateful for them. I am glad my son will carry on their last name, because to me, it represents a family that shows up for one another. A family that cares. A family that feels like what family is supposed to be.

**[pbu08-0317]** — Reader context: Mother announces arrival with two dogs; partner proposes two weeks without visitors.

> I'll try to keep this short. I'm almost 35 weeks pregnant and I received a cold out of the blue text from my mom yesterday that she will be coming to our house (2.5 hr drive) as soon as I'm in labor. She said since it will be a sudden trip she will have to bring her two golden retrievers (poorly trained 1 and 2 year olds). I have a reactive dog towards other dogs, so that means I would have to block off my basement for her dogs and keep my dog separate upstairs where me and the baby will be. I told her absolutely not. My husband is very upset that she put this on me without a discussion and is now adamant about no visitors at all for 2 weeks post birth. He will have 2-3 weeks paternity leave and will completely care for me and baby.

**[pbu08-0355]** — Reader context: Infertile relative's silence feels hurtful despite understanding her difficulty.

> My issue is that after that day, she has not written once asking how I am, I send them updates, with her just replying with short messages. I tried to set a visit, but they are “busy for months”. I am starting to feel resentment, because although I understand this is difficult for her I also have friends and family who have and are struggling with multiple miscarriages and still write for checks on me and the baby, and ask to plan baby showers for us. My husband is also very sad, this is his only sister, and she is acting as if this moment and us are not important. He wants to talk to her, but finds it difficult.

**[pbu08-0364]** — Reader context: Feels pregnancy has erased years of progress in being kinder to herself.

> Idk. What can I do to feel better? Be easier on myself? Give myself a break? HAHA I've never had that skill and I've been combating it for years in therapy and it has gotten better but this right now feels like it erased all my progress.

**[pbu08-0376]** — Reader context: Offers an infertile relative control over receiving updates, then gives space.

> I wrote to her saying that I feel we could have been more sensitive about our announcement, but we were so happy and excited we didn't think it through, and I asked her if she wanted to be involved moving forward, saying I love her and support her with any decision made. She wrote she was having a hard time accepting that this might not be in the books for her, and I took that as a note to give her space. Up until here, everything was okey.

**[pbu21-0004]** — Reader context: Asks how to announce pregnancy after a relative’s child dies.

> I was planning on announcing at a mother’s day lunch. Would it be better if I approach them before with this news ?

**[pbu21-0168]** — Reader context: Wants to tell partner but fears raising hope too early.

> I literally haven’t even told my husband yet as he’s been out of town on work travel. He returns tonight and I really want to tell him but I don’t want to get his hopes up. I don’t think I can keep this inside me though!!! Hence, here I am on Reddit.

**[pbu21-0232]** — Reader context: Bleeding after first positive test triggers blame for telling parents early.

> One problem: I started bleeding today. It looks just like a period and I’m wearing a pad. I don’t have any cramps yet whatsoever but I’m upset. I blame myself a little bit because I feel like I told my parents too early and I’m also a very anxious person so I kept worrying about miscarrying.

**[pbu21-0238]** — Reader context: Family’s muted response after another relative’s losses leaves author feeling wrong.

> I have directly told her multiple times that I want to include her in our family without making things harder on her. The times she responded she says she wants to be included and it’s ok that we send updates about the baby to the family group chat. Yet every time we do we are met with zero response from her and the parents’ muted responses. It makes me feel anxious and feel like I’m doing something wrong, because they are a family that bottles everything up until people just stop talking to eachother forever. I am from a loud and open family and this dynamic drives me crazy. (I’m also probably a bit neurodivergent and don’t always pick up on subtle communication cues. I tend to just stop engaging with people as a rejection avoidance thing when they appear to not like my energy or ignore me repeatedly.)

**[pbu21-0263]** — Reader context: Partner wants to disclose for support while author feels anxious after uncertain findings.

> My husband still wants to tell his parents this weekend which is fair because even if we lost the baby we would want their support but Im suddenly feeling anxious about it. Ive already told my sister and  few close friends last week but that was before I knew what I know now.

**[pbu21-0265]** — Reader context: Delayed announcement out of care for a grieving friend prompts unwanted family inquiries.

> We’ve told close friends and family, but haven’t shared on social media yet - partly due to just liking to keep things sacred and special for a bit, and then also a good friend just experienced a miscarriage and I didn’t want to add salt to her wound. However, I posted a photo to social media of me wearing an empire-style dress at a wedding where I do look pregnant due to the angle and the way the dress flowed. Several people have texted my mom and sister asking if I’m pregnant… this felt so strange and not well-mannered to me?! Of course I’ve assumed someone is pregnant if I see a certain photo, but have never thought to text their family and bluntly ask. It would also be terrible on the off-chance they’re not pregnant!

**[pbu21-0287]** — Reader context: Naming a baby becomes a negotiation over which parent will feel excluded.

> I do also have a stepdad who is amazing, and I feel like he would be so happy if he was included. But then I can’t include him because it might upset my Dad. And I worry if I use my Dad’s name, my mom / stepdad are going to be upset.

**[pbu21-0333]** — Reader context: Fears relatives sharing new address with estranged father.

> This scares me a bit because like I said id heard from him more during the time leading up to the wedding than I had in years, I can only imagine what hell do now that there is a grandchild. I really dont know what to do, do I tell him and just keep him at a distance? We just moved so he doesnt have our address, but one of my little brothers lives with me, not the one that is close with our dad but he is close with the brother that IS close with our dad. Hes already asked me if he can give that brother our address to come over and help him with his car, I havnt responded yet but im.scared the brother close to bio dad will give that information to him.

**[pbu32-0096]** — Reader context: Sixteen months postpartum; says lack of interest began at birth.

> My sister (33) who I was very close with before children never ever asks about my daughter. Like not once since I have had her. She has also never unprompted came to visit her, but hey, my expectations are much lower than that.

**[pbu32-0122]** — Reader context: In-laws' announcement plans conflict with pregnant author's privacy.

> This really upset me because I want to keep it a really private affair and not let everyone know. my husband tried to make her understand but she said since they live close by to all the relatives she would have to tell everyone.

**[pbu32-0210]** — Reader context: Wants to share immediately but believes announcement should wait until twelve weeks.

> I needed to tell someone outside of my husband as it’s driving me crazy keeping this monumental secret and it’s only been a day lol so beyond excited to be a mom 🥹

**[pbu32-0218]** — Reader context: Reassuring eight-week scan does not stop worry before twelve-week scan.

> Some people in my small town have found out I’m pregnant, and my brain is telling me that it’s going to jinx it, and I will get bad news at my next scan.

**[pbu32-0222]** — Reader context: Family conflict at five weeks makes happy announcement imagery painful.

> I see all of these pregnancy announcements and ultra happy reactions on Instagram and, while I should know better, I just feel sad and envious that I don’t have that.

**[pbu32-0262]** — Reader context: Earlier announcement timing makes new pregnancy hard to share.

> We had our mc literally the day after telling our entire family so I’m feeling very guarded with that but at the same time I want to enjoy every millisecond of it

### Additional evidence for pattern 11: Stillbirth and neonatal death include birth, pain relief, recovery, clothes, keepsakes, death paperwork and leaving without the baby.

**[pbu08-0392]** — Reader context: During another pregnancy, asks whether earlier miscarriage contractions resemble labor.

> The whole experience felt very physical and almost labor-like to me — especially the waves of pain followed by actually passing the pregnancy. I’m pregnant again now and have been wondering whether what I experienced was at all similar to actual labor, just on a different scale.

**[pbu21-0078]** — Reader context: Hemorrhage and pregnancy loss preceded family silence.

> Then, when I was 5 months postpartum, I found out I was pregnant again. Complete accident, and we didn’t tell anybody but my parents. A month ago, I hemorrhaged, literally almost died, I lost a ton of blood, couldn’t stay conscious, had to receive 3 units of blood just to get my hemoglobin to 8, and gave birth to my 12 week old baby. Literally the most traumatic thing my husband and I have ever experienced. My husband told his entire family. Sent pictures of our perfect little baby boys feet and hands to the family group chat.

**[pbu32-0065]** — Reader context: Looks back two years after missed miscarriage at twelve-week scan.

> I was sent home with no ultrasound pictures, no proof of the love we had carried was real the past 8 weeks of knowing I was pregnant, and a medication to move on.

**[pbu32-0066]** — Reader context: Author's persistent self-blame about medication after diagnosed missed miscarriage; later healthy child has not erased grief.

> Everyone says there's nothing I could have done differently. I still feel like maybe if I didn't take it what if there was life enough to get that tiny heart beat going. I still feel wrong for ending the little life prematurely.

### Additional evidence for pattern 12: Birth preferences weigh previous injury, pain, safety, anxiety and future fertility, and can change before or during labor.

**[pbu08-0005]** — Reader context: Prior infant death makes this pregnancy frightening.

> I had a son pass away from SIDS in 2021 and I was nervous but happy at the thought of a girl. I had my full anatomy ultrasound yesterday and we all saw that it was very obviously a boy.

**[pbu08-0130]** — Reader context: During emergency induction and section, feels warnings that something was wrong were ignored.

> I’m sorry in advance if this post is all over the place. I’m just so exhausted and done. I had a traumatic labour induction experience that escalated into an emergency C-section. I had uterine hyperstimulation and my baby’s heart rate dropped. I felt like I wasn’t being listened to when I kept saying something was wrong.

**[pbu08-0194]** — Reader context: Possible early induction comes with guilt that the body is failing the baby.

> All this to say… baby may come sooner than we think. I may have to be induced anywhere between 36-38 weeks. Sooner if I have preeclampsia.
> 
> I have so much on my shoulders right now and I’m trying so hard not to stress about it. Part of me feels guilty because of the fact that my body is partially failing my baby, and I know thats not true because this is all due to pregnancy hormones, but I can’t help feel this way.

**[pbu08-0206]** — Reader context: Preparation cannot remove fears of clots or preterm labor while flying.

> I vacillate between being really scared and being confident everything will be completely fine. I am fully prepared as much as I can be, but of course I really do not want a blood clot or pre term labor.

**[pbu08-0369]** — Reader context: After unexplained bleeding and reassuring discharge, monitors constantly and fears ordinary activity.

> I’m extremely grateful that my baby is as healthy as can be and I’m doing good! With all things considered, truly the best outcome happened but I’m still so bothered by the random bleeding and that we couldn’t figure out why it happened. They said it could be from a number of things but I guess nothing serious since it stopped and they couldn’t find a cause? I’m just extremely paranoid now… Still wearing a pad, I check for blood on it every time I pee, any weird sensation feeling I run to the bathroom to check, if I don’t feel my baby enough I start to overthink her movements, I’m scared to move too much or do things around the house, feeling like I need to be in bubble wrap, I’m even scared to poop/push too hard like I’m gonna rupture something in there lol

**[pbu21-0085]** — Reader context: Grieves both the birth she wanted and an abrupt end to pregnancy.

> At the same time, even though the pregnancy was difficult, I felt beautiful. I took really good care of myself, enjoyed having a bump, traveled, and really made the most of the time before having the baby.  
> Now I feel like I’m grieving the birth I didn’t get to have, and also grieving the fact that I never got to properly say goodbye to my pregnancy.

**[pbu21-0090]** — Reader context: Partner helped early after surgery but later missed the baby’s sleep needs.

> The issue is my husband plays with my baby and doesn’t get the whole wake window and importance of nap times until it is too late and i get an overtired baby at the end of his time with him but he has used a bottle with him before when i had procedures or dr appointments and he has helped a-lot in the first weeks especially since i had a c-section

**[pbu21-0099]** — Reader context: Reports choosing unmedicated births because of epidural phobia.

> So I’ve given birth 3 times (unmedicated because I have a phobia around epidurals) and never experienced postpartum hair loss.

**[pbu21-0163]** — Reader context: Existing back injury intensifies fear of childbirth pain.

> My main fear is the birthing, what if there's complications? How much pain will I be in, since I already struggle with back pain. Literally at 6 weeks, I pulled my lower back I was bed ridden just because I lifted up a pot of water for pasta.

**[pbu21-0194]** — Reader context: Reports leaving against medical advice to rest and prepare for VBAC.

> I tell her I am signing myself out AMA. My plan is to not go back to work so I can relax and prepare myself for a VBAC. I have a blood pressure cuff and my baby is active. It feels like she wants to break out of there so I’m not worried about kicks. I can watch my diet and eat less French fries which I have been having almost daily.

**[pbu21-0202]** — Reader context: Proposed membrane sweep reflects current illness and prior blood loss.

> Main reasons my doctor wants to do this are that I have had excessive weight gain this pregnancy (no GD or hypertension), have a family history of large babies, had large amount of blood loss with my first, and have been chronically ill (back to back sicknesses and pneumonia) this entire pregnancy.

**[pbu21-0213]** — Reader context: Postpartum relationship stories frighten someone with a supportive partner.

> There is no one else in the world I’d want to have a family with, but I’m so scared as I keep hearing scary stories of people breaking up, having their relationships fall apart, the painful distance between each other and my partner and I often are around each other even though when I was working he worked nights and I worked mornings. We spend a lot of time together and have for years, he’s my best friend. I’m scared I will fail as a mother as well, but I work on myself every day to be better. I’m afraid for the slew deprivation even though it’s nothing I’m not used to, I’m just so afraid. I feel so many worries popping up, but does anyone have any comforting advice, happy stories, or even just positive things? I’m sorry if my post is a bit messy, I am sooo freaking tired cause the first trimester like insomnia and stuff is AWFUL!!

**[pbu21-0225]** — Reader context: Prior loss colors anticipation of returning to the delivery room.

> I wanted another baby for 6 years, got pregnant last year and lost him at 16 weeks. My last delivery room experience was the saddest day of my life and my brain hasn’t quite gotten itself wrapped around the idea that this time is going to be different. I’m subconsciously preparing myself for something to go wrong more than I’m allowing myself to be excited. I’ve done that my entire pregnancy so far. I hold my breath at appointments just to be told everything looks great and baby is doing wonderfully.

**[pbu21-0239]** — Reader context: Approaching birth intensifies longing for deceased grandmother.

> I was raised by my grandmother who was the best person, she really and truly loved me. She unfortunately passed away before I became pregnant and I have nobody else I would want with me in such a time. My mother wasn’t really a mother and we don’t have a good relationship. I have no female friends in my country and my husbands family isn’t an option either, in fact we bonded a lot over the fact that we are pretty much alone if that makes any sense. Anyway idk it’s probably just the pregnancy hormones but every now and then I get super upset and miss my grandmother more than anything because I just want her here with me in this time and I feel so stupid and alone.

**[pbu21-0318]** — Reader context: Sudden high-risk status changes location, appointments and delivery timing.

**[pbu21-0326]** — Reader context: Asks whether one-sided pain is normal and how to manage anxiety.

> Recently, I have been experiencing some cramping that feels almost like a somewhat light-burning pulling or tension over my right ovary. Not as severe as the ovulation pain, but in the same area. I haven’t had any spotting or other issues. Is this something normal or something that others have experienced? Additionally, how do you deal with the anxiety surrounding the cramping and early pregnancy risks?

**[pbu21-0348]** — Reader context: Relative’s death after childbirth intensifies fear of lost control.

> Beyond the fear of physical pain, what scares me most is the total loss of control and the sheer uncertainty of birth. I’m over 35, and had a cousin who literally passed away after childbirth which has left me with deep trauma and severe health anxiety. I don't know how I’m going to find the strength to pull through. Sometimes I feel so sad for my baby girl for choosing someone weak like I am as her mom.

**[pbu32-0023]** — Reader context: After severe HG and medically recommended termination; fears another pregnancy.

**[pbu32-0040]** — Reader context: At thirty-six weeks, lab results may move induction from next week to this week.

**[pbu32-0231]** — Reader context: Supportive fiancé and housing plan coexist with medical anxiety after unexpected positive test.

**[pbu32-0277]** — Reader context: Returning abroad to give birth means leaving supportive husband for noisy, conflict-filled parental home.

> Now, I have to be there for a minimum of 4 months, and most of this time my husband won't be with me.

**[pbu32-0298]** — Reader context: Bicornuate uterus diagnosis after preterm birth and nineteen-week loss; seeks comparable experiences.

### Additional evidence for pattern 13: An emergency can replace the expected date, team and hospital and separate the parent, baby and partner.

**[pbu32-0099]** — Reader context: Rejects resilience language after multiple surgeries, separation and forty-day hospital stay.

**[pbu08-0031]** — Reader context: Blood-pressure concerns affect travel plans to visit a dying grandmother.

> We have considered flying but I've run into an issue with high blood pressure. It's been normal at home, but there have been a couple of appointments now where I can't get it down. I've been seeing an MFM for two months. I've yet to discuss flying but of course, I will. However, I would really like to keep my stress down (and blood pressure).

**[pbu08-0119]** — Reader context: Repeated triage visits and a recent scan do not calm symptom and movement anxiety.

> So during pregnancy I got diagnosed with pregnancy anxiety and ocd. it’s been fine so far trying to mange and I avoid triggers at all costs and make sure I stay on top of health as much as I can. But suddenly my physical symptoms have caused me even more anxiety. I’m sure my belly is stretching so having round ligament pains which makes me anxious and nervous from the pains. I’ve also already been to triage 3 times for movements and despite the scan 2 weeks ago I still feel scared something is gonna happen it’s like my brain just doesn’t wanna relax. had a few Braxton hicks too which are painful but Thank god they pass and it’s not actually labour yet I’ve still got 9 weeks to go and I just get so scared over everything all the time. Anyone else dealing with this much anxiety and depression during the final trimester?

**[pbu08-0214]** — Reader context: Childhood labeling as dramatic undermines confidence in judging pregnancy symptoms.

> I was one of those sensitive little girls who got told I was dramatic A LOT growing up. Now I’m an adult who doesn’t trust my own judgement and struggles to understand if my actions are coming from a logical place or an emotional place.

**[pbu08-0234]** — Reader context: Researching itching finds a rare early case while the doctor appointment is days away.

> Of course I googled and now I’m worried about cholestasis/ICP, although I know it’s more common later in pregnancy. But I have read a few research papers and read a case study where mother developed it when she was only 5W pregnant. I can’t see my doctor for a few more days

**[pbu08-0278]** — Reader context: The term threatened miscarriage frightens despite understanding it describes early bleeding.

> The ER doctor used the term “threatened miscarriage,” which honestly scared me. I know that’s a medical term used whenever there’s bleeding in early pregnancy, but I’m still really anxious.  
> **Has anyone else had light spotting this early in pregnancy and gone on to have a healthy pregnancy?** I’d really appreciate hearing your experiences while I wait for my repeat hCG results.

**[pbu08-0287]** — Reader context: Surprised by a class rather than an appointment and blamed for harming the baby.

> First thing out of the doctor’s mouth when she walked in was “oh… you brought your kids.” Yeah, I wouldn’t have if I was actually told what this “appointment” was actually going to be. About 10 minutes go by and she starts telling me “your pancreas is pretty much done and it can never go back to being normal. That being said, you’re destroying your baby’s pancreas as well before they’re even born. You’re also going to have complications and might need a c section or induction as well.”

**[pbu08-0291]** — Reader context: Questions whether precautions reflect a concerning short cervix or extra caution.

> I’m a bit confused because I thought the cervix naturally shortens as pregnancy progresses. Is this amount of shortening considered concerning, or is my doctor just being extra cautious? Has anyone had a similar pattern and still made it to term or close to term?

**[pbu08-0302]** — Reader context: Healthy children do not erase grief or the lack of an explanation for repeated losses.

> I’m devastated. I know I have two beautiful healthy kids but it’s still a hard thing to go through. I’m looking for stories of anyone with similar HCG levels and what the outcome was, anyone that’s had miscarriages like this that have had healthy outcomes? I’ve been to a fertility clinic and they found nothing wrong. My miscarriages can’t be explained.

**[pbu08-0304]** — Reader context: At thirty-four weeks, a second-opinion appointment triggers the usual OB's refusal to continue care.

> Flash forward to yesterday. I have an appointment with my original OB. I have had this scheduled for over two weeks. I get a call an hour before my appointment saying it was cancelled. Because apparently this OB has a no-take-back policy. Because I scheduled an appointment with another OB, even though I wasn't actually seen, my original OB won't see me. I explained the situation to the staff person on the phone (that I wasn't actually seen and didn't actually transfer care), and she got the office manager who reiterated that OB wouldn't see me bc I made the appointment. She also insinuated that I was lying bc they could see in my chart that the other OB had my information Bc they were all part of the same network. I explained (for the third time!) that I did go to the appointment, was not seen, did not establish care, but of course they had access to my information because they were (as she said) part of the same network. Manager said there were no exceptions, OB wouldn't see me. I asked what I was supposed to do--I'm 34w, high-risk, and was supposed to talk about induction at this appointment. Manager said she could give me the number of the OB I tried to see. I declined and hung up.

**[pbu08-0319]** — Reader context: Search results intensify worry about discharge before an appointment weeks away.

> Hi! Need some guidance— I am 4w4d, and I have brown discharge now. I don’t know if I should be concerned. I did not have any cramping but of course Google is making me crazy by saying it could be an early sign of ectopic pregnancy or miscarriage. Not sure what to do? I have an 8 week appointment August 24th. 
> 
> Any suggestions or positive stories? Worried as hell.

**[pbu08-0322]** — Reader context: After loss, needs to know normal symptoms before deciding whether to go to the ER.

> hi! I'm 13 weeks with my second pregnancy. I lost my first at 10 weeks due to my septate uterus. I've had my fair share of scares this time around mostly due to paranoia from my first loss. Tonight, I've had some uncomfortable cramping in like a very localized spot below my belly button and to the right a tiny bit. It's worse when I lay on my right side or move aggressively. I know my body is doing crazy things rn and it's gonna be uncomfortable, but I truly over analyze every single symptom and will not be able to have peace unless I know what's normal and what isn't.

**[pbu08-0326]** — Reader context: Embarrassment about an umbrella injury competes with the wish to seek reassurance.

> My baby is moving normally, and I haven’t had any bleeding or leaking fluid, but I’m still really scared. Has anyone experienced something similar? Did you go to Labor & Delivery or the ER? Would you get checked just to be safe?  
> I feel a little embarrassed because it was “just an umbrella,” but the impact felt much stronger than I expected.

**[pbu08-0328]** — Reader context: Spoiled juice triggers contamination fears and debate over calling before a scheduled scan.

> I’m currently 7w6d pregnant, and I realized today that the prune juice I drank was definitely bad, it smelled and tasted metallic. I had 8oz this morning and 8oz yesterday but didn’t notice any strange taste/smell yesterday. I read on the plastic bottle today that it needs to be consumed 7-10 days after opening. Embarrassingly, it has been opened in our fridge for at least 9 months. I noticed no symptoms of being sick yesterday. I’m so worried now that the juice has grown listeria or salmonella or something that will hurt my baby… I have my first ultrasound in 2 days. I did end up throwing up after drinking this and eating breakfast because of morning sickness but definitely didn’t throw it all up. I’ve been trying to drink a ton of water today to try and flush it all out. Any advice??? Don’t even think it’s worth calling my OB about since I’m going in Monday for a scan. Just so anxiety inducing, especially after multiple losses. Thanks in advance.

**[pbu08-0353]** — Reader context: Only learns of a previously seen hematoma when calling about bleeding.

**[pbu08-0354]** — Reader context: Delayed scan is at the clinic associated with previous bad news.

**[pbu08-0381]** — Reader context: Unexplained losses and advice to try again leave a persistent search for cause.

**[pbu21-0003]** — Reader context: Friends offer hope while the midwife expects loss.

My poor husband didnt understand why me and the tech got quiet and why I asked if I was seeing what I thought I was seeing. I am sad, embarrassed, and just feeling so...Blah.

**[pbu21-0033]** — Reader context: Therapist’s reassurance leaves author asking whether connection must wait until birth.

**[pbu21-0067]** — Reader context: Post-birth depression, frequent infant appointments and no support lead to seeking Reddit help.

> I just hate that I go through all the fucking shit of being pregnant and pushing a human out of me and breast feeding and having 2+ appts a week for my little girl (who I fucking love more than anything btw) and then I get the fucking joys of having PPA and PPD and finding out the hard way that I have zero fucking support from anyone even my husband who was the one who wanted to have kids originally (no regrets now that I have my angel baby but the point is THE POINT) I’m fucking a solo parent basically and therapists are hard to find and I’m just losing it. Sorry to vent on here but I have no where else to turn. There was a time when I’d use chatGPT but I understand the evils of it now and am left to be here, because I have no one else to turn to.

**[pbu21-0091]** — Reader context: Left a maternal follow-up appointment because the baby became too distressed.

**[pbu21-0149]** — Reader context: Reports being made to search a shared bathroom bin while in labor.

> \- I arrived at the hospital after leaking bright orange liquid to be checked if its in fact my waters and meconium. I was told to bring a used pad or underwear to show the team, which I did. When I arrived the midwife gave me a new bigger pad after I mentioned I am leaking heavier now. Then she claimed that she asked me to save the old one and asked me to go back to the bathroom and try to find it in the bin (??). I kept repeating that I already have a used pad to show that I brought from home but she insisted she needs the one I chucked away and she brought me to the bathroom and told me to put my hand in a completely full bin of a shared bathroom and find it. I was in shock from everything what’s happening so I complied and did not find it. Its shocking to me now that I was asked to do that with my bare hands all while in labour.

**[pbu21-0150]** — Reader context: Doctor allegedly dismisses pain and mocks a prepared medical history.

> \- The epidural was given by a doctor I suspect is also locum who only spoke sarcastically and when I explained my medical history as advised by anesthetist that I had a consultation with before birth his response was : do you spend a lot of time on chat gpt?     
> The epidural did not work for an hour and when midwife brought him back he did not even bother spraying my leg just said I do not look in pain and that he cannot remove feelings of pressure for me. I explained again that I am in fact in pain and can feel everything on one side. He then asked me: if you are in pain why you stopped using gas and air? It pissed me off so much even if I was in the zone and very compliant until this point telling him I must be a liar then. The midwife did not advocate for me at all.

**[pbu21-0151]** — Reader context: Emergency newborn care lacked explanation and ended with perceived blame.

> Again instead of enjoying this most anticipated magical bonding time with my baby I kept thinking about how my baby nearly died because I did not see if they were breathing and everything just seemed so fragile and verging between life and death. This moment of anxiety and shock was interrupted by same midwife turning up the ward to say that we were amazing clients ( I ignored all of it and just wanted her to be out) she then looked at me and said : “you forgot how to correctly hold a baby didn’t you” and walked away. Basically implying I caused my baby to stop breathing which from what I understand now was very unlikely have been caused by me, even if it was the position they were in it was the midwife that put my baby on me like that.

**[pbu08-0027]** — Reader context: Twice-weekly therapy and newly started medication coexist with isolation.

The concept of asking for help feels beyond me, and I just have seen so many different experiences from other people and I’m like why is everyone else getting offered so much help and love and support and I’m not?

**[pbu08-0106]** — Reader context: Late recognition triggers fear about alcohol, smoking and recreational drug exposure.

>

**[pbu08-0174]** — Reader context: Gym helps mood, but supplement uncertainty sparks fears about the baby.

But i started a spiral of thoughts about if im keeping water from my not even formed baby lol

**[pbu08-0193]** — Reader context: Different clinicians change medication plans while test results remain pending.

> My high risk doc stopped the BP meds and the meds for Cholestasis this day as well. After looking at the rashes on my belly hands and arms, she suspected it was more likely to be PUPP rashes than Cholestasis. She didn’t want me on meds for something that it (the rashes) may not be. She didn’t want me on BP meds either because she wants to see if I have gestational hypertension or if I’m at risk of developing preeclampsia. She has me monitoring my BP at home and ordered labs to check this.

**[pbu08-0235]** — Reader context: Later testing and referral follow the original early-pregnancy itching question.

> Yeah I got tested. My bile acids are high at 21 ( fasting) and ALT and GGT are abnormal as well. My GP has started me on the Ursodiol and referred me to maternity assessment unit in the hospital; still waiting to be seen.

**[pbu08-0245]** — Reader context: Author names a supplement brand and regimen as a reported UTI remedy; not verified treatment advice.

> •Take D-Mannose: I used the Uqora brand. I recommend the regular flush (it’s like a lemonade flavor! the advanced formula made me a little nauseous, and the berry flavor is not great) take twice a day.

**[pbu08-0246]** — Reader context: Attributes changing home test-strip readings to supplements; efficacy is the author's belief.

> I was using test strips over the course of a week and the amount of bacteria kept lessening with D-mannose, but I stalled out around 4 days of taking. So then I added
> 
> •Activated Charcoal supplements right before bed, at least two hours after your last D-mannose dose so that has a chance to get into your system and get to work. Charcoal binds toxins and bacteria and helps flush them out of your body.
> 
> First night taking charcoal led to only trace amounts of bacteria on my next uti test strip. Second night had no detected bacteria on the test. 
> 
> I truly believe the charcoal helped kick the rest.

**[pbu08-0280]** — Reader context: Medication timing means enduring morning heartburn while waiting to take antacids.

> I have pregnancy anemia and so I have to take iron pills but I can’t take them with calcium because they won’t absorb right which means I have to suffer through heart burn most mornings until the two to three hour mark when I can finally take tums.

**[pbu08-0285]** — Reader context: After ectopic treatment asks about alcohol restrictions during a holiday.

> I’ve recently gotten the mtx shot days prior for ectopic pregnancy and I’m wondering if the risks are really that high with avoiding alcohol, it is the 4th of July and I would like to have a few drinks given what I’ve gone through this past week and to also celebrate the 4th, please let me know if I’m really going to put much damage on my liver having a few %5 alcoholic drinks, thank you!

**[pbu08-0303]** — Reader context: Conflicting clinician advice changes blood-pressure medication use and the author reports worsening symptoms.

> my normal OB was not helpful at all, though. She kept being convinced that I actually had low BP and told me multiple times that I could stop taking the BP meds. Finally, I decided to listen to her one day, and had the worst day of my pregnancy so far probably. I had numbers in the 150s/80s, the worst migraine of my life and kept seeing stars. Took my BP meds earlier in the evening and started taking them 2x day at the advice of my MFM. My BP has been great since then, but it was super frustrating to have my normal OB disregard my actual symptoms (headaches, dizziness, extreme fatigue after minor exercise, with numbers in 130s and 140s) bc her own BP cuff was messed up during her pregnancy.

**[pbu08-0310]** — Reader context: Different symptoms after changed IVF medication are compared with two similar previous pregnancies.

> I am nearly 6 weeks pregnant and have my first scan next friday, and I dont feel the same symptoms, I only get hungry at night, no morning sickness so far, and only tender breasts. 
> 
> They are all ivf pregnancies but after my loss we changed up the medication this time around. I even used to get heartburn from the very start of pregnancy, like the moment there is a pregnancy I know because I usually have heartburn. 
> 
> For people who have had multiple pregnancies, have your symptoms changed from one pregnancy to another? Even with first two being almost identical symptoms?

**[pbu08-0331]** — Reader context: Anesthesia worries coexist with inability to function while passing kidney stones.

> Just wanted to see if anyone has experienced this? Of course I worry about the anesthesia but I’m not able to function trying to pass them on my own :(

**[pbu08-0358]** — Reader context: Symptoms attributed to SSRI withdrawal and weight-loss injections delay recognition of an unexpected pregnancy.

> 
> I have also been tapering down my SSRI. Been off it for 2 weeks and the withdrawl right now is intense. Awful dizziness and brain zaps.
> 
> I attributed all symptoms to the withdrawl or the jabs, I only took a test because I wanted to go through the motions at a Dr appointment where I wanted something for the withdrawl side effects...
> 
> There doesn't seem to be much hope for this pregnancy with all the negative factors, but I think ill feel really crushed if it doesn't work out...

**[pbu08-0359]** — Reader context: Stops previously effective allergy medicine because of pregnancy and seeks alternatives.

I've been allergic to several trees, grass, and ragweed for a long time now, and 2 years ago found a medication that helps me feel 85% normal during even the worst of my allergy seasons. Buuut now I'm pregnant and none of the hardcore allergy meds are tested on pregnant women so of course I had to go off it.

**[pbu08-0362]** — Reader context: Fever and over-the-counter medicines before recognition prompt worry about whether early care is available.

> Not sure if a doctor would even see me to answer these questions this early on, but would my fever harm my baby? Would the medication harm the baby? Google is freaking me out.

**[pbu08-0373]** — Reader context: Reports nausea preventing SSRI use, substance-based coping after loss and plans for further treatment.

> After the miscarriage I started using thc to help come to terms with everything. I also have a history of reoccurring severe MDD and SI which started acting up after my hysteroscopy. I tried restarting my lexapro during my first pregnancy but I got too nauseous and I have to be on a higher dose inorder for it to work so I had to stop taking it. I knew the only thing that could help get me out of the SI feedback loop would be psilocybin (decriminalized here) so I was able to find some and it did get me out of the dark place I was in. I no longer felt like it was going to end one of two ways. Only thing is I think I’m probably going to have to look into getting a prescription for Ketamine for treatment resistant depression because after some time I feel like I’m going to start getting bad again unless I get on medication maintenance.

**[pbu08-0380]** — Reader context: Fear of medication effects delays asking for help.

> I haven’t been to the doctor yet because I’m scared of being given more medication and worried about how anything I’m already on could affect the baby’s development. But I’m feeling really lost and unsure what to do.

**[pbu08-0390]** — Reader context: Had planned a year of stability off medication before conception.

> My mental health is another important factor. I have been off mental health medication for 4 months, and before this pregnancy I had wanted to be stable for a full year without medication before deliberately trying to become pregnant.

**[pbu08-0407]** — Reader context: Immediately stops vaping after pregnancy recognition but fears damage following a prior loss.

> As the title says I am 6weeks and 5 days pregnant and have been vaping without knowing I was pregnant I’m worried I’ve already caused damage any advice on this I’m stopping today but I am so scared I had a miscarriage about 4 months ago and stopped immediately, going to do the same but I’m scared I’ve already messed things up for my baby !

**[pbu21-0027]** — Reader context: Fears that feeling emotions will harm the baby.

**[pbu21-0119]** — Reader context: Describes multiple treatments and temporary relief for a pregnancy rash.

> I tried **everything** I could think of — pine tar soap, calamine lotion (which gave temporary relief), ice packs, cold flannels, emollients and hydrocortisone prescribed by my GP.  
> Honestly, nothing seemed to give me lasting relief.  
> Eventually, when the itching became unbearable, I went to maternity triage. They checked me for **intrahepatic cholestasis of pregnancy (ICP)**, which thankfully was ruled out. I was also prescribed an antihistamine four times a day, which helped calm the itching temporarily.

**[pbu21-0120]** — Reader context: Reports rapid relief from a family remedy; one crossposted author account.

> Bless her ❤️ — she suggested a very simple* des*i remedy that she had used/known about:** fine powdered alum (fitkari) mixed with coconut oil to make a slurry/past**e, which I applied to the affected areas.  
> I honestly couldn’t believe it — **within around 10 minutes, the itching felt dramatically calmer.** With continued use, my skin also started improving and the rash eventually settled.

**[pbu21-0248]** — Reader context: Normal results and medication do not remove expectation of loss.

**[pbu08-0009]** — Reader context: Severe itching disrupts sleep while waiting for test results.

> I got my blood tested a week ago. Still waiting on my bile acid. My liver enzymes were fine, but the itching is SO intense that my feet burn from scratching. It's not just on my palms and soles, but my fingers, toes, side of feet, etc. It wakes me up in the middle of the night and the only relief I get is from cold water. I have caused bumps ALL over my hands and feet from scratching. NOTE - they were not there before. I have no rash. Just irritation from constant itching.

**[pbu08-0211]** — Reader context: Denied requested sleep, asks whether exhaustion and hurt are being dismissed as hormonal.

And then to top it off, he doesn't spend any time with me and prioritizes his phone the entire time.. once I get home and make dinner, he goes and puts the sabres game on and ignores me the rest of the night..
> 
> I voiced how I'm feeling, I've explained my exhaustion and he just sits there with an "I don't care" attitude. 
> 
> He is acting like I am completely unreasonable, hormonal, and irrational for being upset and hurt by all of this. His excuse is "I'm sick"..... Meanwhile I've been sick for over a month and still expected to show up for everyone.

**[pbu08-0236]** — Reader context: Reports immediate improvement in sleep and nausea with Unisom.

I’ve had on and off issues with some amount of insomnia my whole life, and now I’m sleeping better than I ever have and it’s incredible. No more nausea.

**[pbu08-0299]** — Reader context: A third pregnancy brings an unfamiliar tight sensation despite previous experience.

**[pbu08-0306]** — Reader context: Changing nausea and breast soreness create uncertainty about what to expect.

I am just nervous because this is my first pregnancy and don’t know what to expect/terrified of miscarriage.

**[pbu08-0341]** — Reader context: Tries to explain stabbing pain as a growth spurt but lacks prior experience.

**[pbu21-0101]** — Reader context: Nausea feels hopeful after two pregnancies without it ended in loss.

When I realised the lettuce I removed it from burger after a few bites. But I've been scared thinking this eating a burger reheated in microwave due to it having mayo and lettuce will make me miscarry.

**[pbu21-0115]** — Reader context: Sudden relief from nausea is frightening rather than welcome.

**[pbu21-0222]** — Reader context: Symptoms and long shifts replace usual hygiene, social contact and daily functioning.

> i really hate being pregnant. today i’m 8w 4d i can’t do anything that i want to do, can’t eat what i want, can hardly drink any fluids without it bothering my stomach and making me feel weird and nauseous. and every time i stand i feel like i have to vomit. i usually shower and clean my room daily and now im just laying in what feels like my own filth which i would have NEVER been comfortable with before. im losing weight, im supposed to be staying hydrated but even water makes me feel funny. i’m throwing up, im in bed 99% of the time, not talking to anyone and just isolated. if i’m not sleeping im in bed scrolling on my phone endlessly. i just really hate this feeling i don’t feel like myself at all. and dont get me started on work omg. i have to work 12 hr shifts 4 days out the week and by the end of that im so fucking drained you would think i was a zombie. and my off days like i said only consist of me being in bed sleeping. i’m miserable.

**[pbu21-0249]** — Reader context: Wishes for nausea as evidence pregnancy continues.

**[pbu21-0283]** — Reader context: Fear focused on postpartum while insomnia and distress already occur during pregnancy.

**[pbu21-0308]** — Reader context: Low appetite after quitting cannabis leads to reducing physical activity.

> I'm 5'2" and 118 Ibs, and I'm 16 weeks pregnant. During the first trimester, I was very sick and lost weight, with my pregnancy weight being 120-122 Ibs. Now I'm in the second trimester and never feel hungry, and sometimes I even hate the thought of eating. Before I was pregnant, I dealt with this by smoking weed, but of course, I can't now. I probably eat around 700-1000 calories a day, no joke. I've cut back on being active because I don't want to burn more calories than I'm taking in. I don't know what to do. My baby seems fine from blood work and ultrasounds but I'm not sure how things will go if this continues.

I’ve been having nausea every day but haven’t thrown up once, I’d say the most annoying symptom I have right now is extreme fatigue. and the past 4 days I’ve been getting waves of dizziness. I'm scared of something being wrong with my baby or having a miscarriage, starting over so late in life. All the things. I am very early pregnant, only like 5 weeks. I will be having all genetic screenings etc done.  But I am just so scared, and hate that I can't just be happy.

**[pbu08-0167]** — Reader context: Watches symptoms after loss and fears caring for two little children.

> So I miscarried last November and it was an early miscarriage, I was only 6 weeks along but I just found out Im pregnant again. Im probably only 5 weeks along but im so scared that its making it hard to be happy or excited about this. My thoughts keep thinking oh it could happen again or if it does go well how much everything would change. I also think about my son and how it would change for him, hes only 1.5 years old. The panic is setting in on watching for symptoms and how I would handle two little ones.

**[pbu08-0250]** — Reader context: A planned pregnancy becomes a feared prospect of raising another child alone.

> I told him once I have the baby these emotions will change. I love him and want to be with him but I can’t ignore everything I went thru during this pregnancy. I have 3 other kids and he was different from any other guy I talked to and now I’m going thru all this. If we coparent we coparent. I would just be mad cuz we planned the baby and I didn’t wanna raise this one alone.

**[pbu08-0333]** — Reader context: Wanted second pregnancy brings depression and guilt unlike immediate connection with the first.

> I have a 15 month old and I’m 7 weeks pregnant, ever since I found out I’m pregnant Ive become extremely depressed. We were trying to conceive so I don’t know why I feel so sad. I have 0 motivation.. if i didn’t have my daughter I probably wouldn’t get out of bed. I’m constantly crying and I don’t have a connection with this pregnancy yet. I didn’t feel this way with my first.. I was so happy and I had a connection from the start. I feel guilty. My husband works in a different state so he’s not home much. I’ve never had much help besides when he’s home. I feel terrified to have another and I feel like I’m not good enough for my daughter bc I can’t give her my best right now. I’m sure I’m going through prenatal depression I just don’t understand why?

**[pbu08-0357]** — Reader context: Feels calmer in another pregnancy but seeks good outcomes after dating behind and no heartbeat.

> This is our second and generally feel a lot calmer this time around, have definitely been having some nausea in the am/early evenings.
> 
> Went for bloodwork today and will repeat tomorrow and Tuesday (holiday weekend) with another scan Tuesday am.
> 
> Any one have similar and have a positive pregnancy?

**[pbu21-0002]** — Reader context: Early scan uncertainty becomes shame about initial ambivalence.

> I feel so much shame for being so scared and not wanting the baby at first. Im still breastfeeding a toddler and dealing with sleep issues. I wasnt ready to do it all again. But I was still so excited to hold a little squishy newborn again. To see my son be a big brother. To go through those sweet, quite moments with another baby.

**[pbu21-0148]** — Reader context: Imagines another birth restoring missed first moments.

> I sometimes think to have a second child but don't want that to happen again, and also think maybe a second would heal that, even if I had an elective C just so I could experience the first scream, first look, first bath, first hold. It feels stolen, the what ifs, I got the boy swaddled, clean, no noise just open eyes.

**[pbu21-0187]** — Reader context: Asks whether extreme fatigue is normal in a twin pregnancy after miscarriage.

> Miscarried in November and now 4/16 11 weeks pregnant with twins - heard both heartbeats and both strong. I am so unbelievably exhausted. It feels like I could sleep all day long. Can anyone else with twins or even at 11 weeks send some reassurance? Is this normal? Especially with twins?

**[pbu21-0231]** — Reader context: Visible bump forces disclosure before desired privacy ends.

> Has anyone else found themselves announcing before they were comfortable with it because they were obviously showing? Any advice? I feel like I don't really have a choice and some of my privacy is being taken away.

**[pbu21-0299]** — Reader context: Husband who wanted another baby leaves after confirmation.

**[pbu21-0377]** — Reader context: Fears sleep loss and travel stress harmed development despite partner taking over.
