I regret going to see my family so much. I wish I had never left my house.

**[pbu21-0384]** — Reader context: Non-parent friend reports host attributing conflict to being nine months pregnant.

**[pbu21-0390]** — Reader context: Loss anxiety occurs despite no previous miscarriage.

**[pbu32-0038]** — Reader context: Friend's fertility grief limits support for author's new pregnancy.

**[pbu32-0061]** — Reader context: Fourteen-hour flight with toddler for bereavement; fears traveling without help.

**[pbu32-0100]** — Reader context: Prior winter newborn experience motivates conception timing despite fertility uncertainty.

**[pbu32-0121]** — Reader context: Two inconclusive fetal-sex tests trigger loss fear before reassuring ultrasound.

**[pbu32-0151]** — Reader context: Wanted pregnancy after eighteen months trying brings anhedonia rather than happiness.

> Nothing I enjoyed before is enjoyable now, all my hobbies are just pointless and not fun and I can’t spend more than 10 minutes on them. I have a 3 year old, who I feel so bad for because I’m just snapping for no reason, I can’t play with for more than 10 minutes.

**[pbu32-0176]** — Reader context: Eighth pregnancy; reduced nausea alarms author despite reassuring early tests and scan.

**[pbu32-0200]** — Reader context: Wanted another boy; believes grief for deceased mother may affect feelings about daughter.

**[pbu32-0282]** — Reader context: Fourth pregnancy with delayed appointment after insurance issues; hides fears prompted by symptoms stopping.

**[pbu21-0372]** — Reader context: Online loss stories delay names and car-seat research.

I have never been pregnant before, but the more I join groups Facebook and Reddit  and get content on social media around pregnancy I see so many heartbreaking miscarriage stories that happen out of nowhere and at all times of the pregnancy and it makes me so anxious. Im so anxious I’ve even been scared to start looking at names or researching into car seats and all of that because I’m scared it will go away. I don’t want it to I want this baby so bad.

**[pbu08-0050]** — Reader context: Distress disrupts eating, work and activity.

**[pbu08-0067]** — Reader context: Reassuring examination and prescribed leave do not stop fear about ongoing spotting.

>

> 
> Should I be worried that I’ll miscarry?

**[pbu08-0080]** — Reader context: Pregnant working parent imagines single motherhood as relief from the current partnership.

> WFHM to a 15 month old plus 5 months pregnant and I am just over my being with my boyfriend. He is not a bad dad in the general sense but not super helpful. I fear I am picturing myself being a single mom and would be more happy.

**[pbu08-0176]** — Reader context: Despite improving symptoms and returning hobbies, excited pregnancy preoccupation makes time crawl.

> I am 14 weeks and a FTM and still cannot think of anything else besides this pregnancy! At first I figured it was because this is my second pregnancy after a loss and because I was so sick during my first trimester. Now I am feeling much better and still cannot focus on anything else! I am starting to incorporate exercise again as well as trying to focus on work to make up for all the missed weeks/little productivity during my sickness. I have exciting trips coming up and am getting back into hobbies but I can't seem to fully focus on anything as I am just nonstop thinking of this pregnancy and excitement for October. At this point it is frustrating as it seems to make time go so much slower! I am trying to look forward to things and invest in myself during this time but I just can't. This is all great because I am just so excited to get to this new chapter of life but still!

**[pbu08-0189]** — Reader context: Induction timing collides with house closing, moving and a last-minute change of doctors.

> Did I mention I was diagnosed with gestational diabetes (GD)?? I was managing well on my diet, but my fasting numbers wouldn't allow for that so now I am on insulin....which means I am going to be induced at 39 weeks. A week earlier then I expected. The week of closing on my house. I do not want to be induced... I wanted to go into labor naturally. Did I mention we still **don't have a house in NY???** Now we have to move in with my parents in MA which also means I will be switching doctors to one in MA a WEEK AND 2 DAYS prior to me being 39 weeks. Its seriously comical at this point, absolutely NOTHING has gone to plan. We don't have that baby room or even our own home. Nesting has been non-existent. I just stare at the baby stuff, wondering how I can pack it differently. I love my parents, but they are going through a thing now too and its making it all the more dreadful.

**[pbu08-0198]** — Reader context: Partner withdraws while the author weighs education, solo parenting and another termination.

> I then found out i was pregnant about 2 weeks ago, i told him and he isnt best pleased, he keeps saying things like ‘i dont want this for myself’ and ‘i cant keep running around for other people’ but my life is also changing drastically. I dont want to go through with another termination as it was an all time low for me. But i just feel like he isn’t going to be there or help in anyway way possible. His messages have gone blunt, and he just doesn’t seem interested in wanting to talk to me anymore, im trying to put the effort it, but he never even asks me if im okay or how im feeling, when i make sure to always ask him how he is doing. Im just finishing my Level 4, and im supposed to be going to uni in September, and i dont want to give up my career, i feel like I cant do it on my own, but i really would regret not keeping the baby.

**[pbu08-0231]** — Reader context: Student illness exposure creates pregnancy worry at work.

**[pbu08-0268]** — Reader context: Promised workplace help fails; fears submitting a doctor's accommodation note.

> Currently 28w as an ftm and I’m having panic attacks bc of how stressful work is. I work in a manufacturing plant as an engineer. I had a mental breakdown at 12w and was told I would get help so I’m not doing the job alone. Well that guy wasn’t cut out for the job and took a different job. So I’ve essentially been doing my job as normal. I have a note from my doctor for reduced work responsibilities but i am so scared to bring it to work. Everyone was really supportive when I had issues at 12w but then no change happened.

**[pbu21-0108]** — Reader context: Anxiety prevents work and eating despite restarting medication.

> I’m currently 8w + 1. I’ve had problems with anxiety/panic attacks before but have had a handle on it for the past 6 years or so. However, since about my 6th week my anxiety has ramped up to a level where I can’t control it. I restarted 50mg sertraline last week and will be starting with NHS talking therapies soon but I am struggling day to day. I haven’t been to work for 2 weeks due to this and as a teacher I feel incredibly guilty. Also, my manager is not very supportive. I wake up every morning on the verge of a panic attack and with morning sickness I really struggle to eat anything at all. I’m being sick a couple of times a day but I don’t know if this is due to the pregnancy or my anxiety. I suppose I just wanted to hear if anyone else has experienced similar or could offer some advice. Thank you

I want to self harm but I don't want to.

**[pbu21-0243]** — Reader context: Looks for successful outcomes after spotting resembling prior loss.
